### [1] [T$^2$-Splat: Adaptive Topology Mesh Splatting with Texture Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06974)
*Zhihao Tang,Youjia Zhang,Mingbo Zhao,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: T²-Splat 将网格拓扑调整与外观细化解耦，以紧凑三角网格实现有竞争力的新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于三角形泼溅的新视角合成可对接传统图形管线，但为保留外观细节往往需要过度细分，导致网格复杂度过高。

Method: 使用安全受限德劳内三角剖分（Safe RDT）初始化网格连接关系；通过动态拓扑演化（DTE）的边坍缩和顶点分裂调整三角形分配；固定网格后，利用逐面的残差 Mip 纹理和尺度自适应滤波细化外观。

Result: 在 Mip-NeRF 360、Tanks & Temples 和 DTU 数据集上，方法以紧凑网格取得有竞争力的结果；在 Mip-NeRF 360 上，PSNR 比 MeshSplatting 提高 0.71 dB。压缩实验支持将面数预算降至原始面数的 10%。

Conclusion: 将拓扑演化与纹理细化分离，有助于在渲染质量和网格复杂度之间实现灵活权衡；摘要未给出最低面数预算下的具体质量损失。

Abstract: Triangle-based splatting connects novel view synthesis with conventional graphics pipelines, but often requires excessive tessellation to preserve appearance details. We present T$^2$-Splat, which decouples topology adaptation from appearance refinement. Safe Restricted Delaunay Triangulation (Safe RDT) initializes mesh connectivity. Dynamic Topology Evolution (DTE) then adapts triangle allocation through edge collapse and vertex split operations. Residual per-face Mip-textures then refine appearance on the fixed mesh using scale-adaptive filtering. Experiments on Mip-NeRF 360, Tanks & Temples, and DTU demonstrate competitive results with compact meshes. T$^2$-Splat improves PSNR by 0.71dB over MeshSplatting on Mip-NeRF 360. Additional compression experiments demonstrate flexible control of mesh complexity, supporting face budgets as low as 10% of the original face count. Project page: https://t2-splat.github.io/.

</details>


### [2] [Batch-Conditioned Semantic Anchors for Robust Transductive Adaptation of Vision--Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06993)
*Mohammed Rahman Sherif Khan Mohammad,Ardhendu Behera,Sandip Pradhan,Swagat Kumar,Amr Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JPTA 利用批次可靠性调节语义参考的更新，在适应无标签测试批次的同时抑制语义漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直推式适应方法在稀疏、类别先验偏移或标签覆盖不足的批次上容易漂移；固定文本原型的保守方法则可能适应不足。

Method: 估计批次类别先验和图像侧软原型，构建向批次图像结构迁移的语义参考，并依据批次可靠性调整其影响。

Result: 摘要称，在标准基准、低有效类别数、全类别评估及在线流场景中，JPTA 均优于 TransCLIP 和 StatA；稀疏及先验偏移批次上的提升最大。

Conclusion: 可靠的批次证据可用于重校准语义参考，在纠正有效类别不匹配的同时避免无约束的直推式漂移；摘要未提供具体量化结果。

Abstract: Transductive adaptation improves vision-language models at test time by refining predictions on an unlabeled batch, but current methods face a key trade-off. Aggressive approaches exploit batch structure but can drift when batches are sparse, have prior shift, or cover little of the label space. Conservative methods better preserve CLIP text semantics, but fixed semantic references can under-adapt even when the batch provides strong corrective evidence. We propose JPTA (Joint Prior and Transport Adaptation), a prior-aware framework that casts transductive VLM adaptation as batch-conditioned semantic reference estimation. Instead of treating CLIP text prototypes as fixed priors or freely replaceable, JPTA views them as semantic references that shift toward image-side batch structure only under calibrated evidence. It estimates batch priors and soft image-side prototypes to form transported semantic references whose impact is scaled by batch reliability. Across standard benchmarks, low effective-class regimes, all-class evaluation, and online streams, JPTA consistently outperforms TransCLIP and StatA, with the largest gains on sparse and prior-shifted batches. Anchor-drift, active-support, and absent-class-mass diagnostics show that JPTA fixes effective-class mismatch without unconstrained transductive drift, indicating that robust transductive VLM adaptation should recalibrate semantic references using reliable batch evidence rather than choose between unrestricted transduction and fixed prototypes. Code is available at https://github.com/MR-Sherif/JPTA.git.

</details>


### [3] [TraCE: Trajectory-Aware Concepts for Explainable Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07009)
*Wooil Lee,Junpyo Hong,Jongseo Lee,Tuomas Oikarinen,Tsui-Wei Weng,Jinwoo Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TraCE通过聚类光流轨迹发现涵盖人物、物体及交互的运动概念，在保持任务性能的同时提升视频模型的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频可解释人工智能存在运动来源欠明确问题：基于区域的解释难以区分运动来源，而仅使用单人姿态序列的运动词汇无法覆盖全部来源。

Method: 对训练视频中的光流轨迹聚类，发现来源灵活的运动概念；结合物体和场景概念表示静态上下文，构建类型化概念接口，分别支持动作识别的类别预测和视频问答中由问题引导的概念匹配。

Result: 在六个基准上以五个随机种子评估；相较不可解释基线，动作识别准确率平均下降1.0个百分点，视频问答准确率名义上提高0.4个百分点。运动扰动和概念移除测试分别提供语义方向一致性与决策忠实性的互补证据。

Conclusion: TraCE能够以较小的性能代价提供覆盖多种运动来源的概念解释，并支持通过样本级干预进行针对性模型调试。

Abstract: Video understanding models often rely on motion, yet their explanations can obscure which motion sources support a prediction. We identify motion-source under-specification as a limitation of existing video XAI: region-centric explanations can be source-ambiguous, while motion vocabularies restricted to single-person pose sequences are source-incomplete. We propose TraCE (Trajectory-Aware Concepts for Explainable Video Understanding), a concept-based framework that discovers source-flexible motion concepts by clustering optical-flow trajectories from training videos. These concepts represent dynamic evidence from people, objects, and interactions, while object and scene concepts account for static context. The resulting typed trajectory/object/scene interface supports class prediction for action recognition and question-conditioned concept matching for video question answering (VQA). We evaluate TraCE across five random seeds on six benchmarks: NTU RGB+D Mutual, Chiral SSv2, UCF-101, KTH, TOMATO, and STAR. Relative to non-interpretable baselines, TraCE maintains competitive task performance, with an average accuracy drop of 1.0 points for action recognition and a nominal gain of 0.4 points for VQA. Motion perturbation and concept-removal tests provide complementary evidence of directional semantic alignment and decision faithfulness, while sample-level interventions illustrate targeted model debugging. Our project page is available at https://leewooil.github.io/TraCE_project_page/.

</details>


### [4] [Gaussians Can Subtract: Neural Dissolve Fields for Hybrid Scene Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07049)
*Yiyan Wang,Haohan Liu,Tetsuo Shibuya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gaussolve通过先选择性消隐不一致的网格内容，再用残差高斯补全，提高混合场景重建的视觉质量与渲染效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加法式网格—高斯混合方法主要用高斯补偿缺失内容，难以处理由几何误差、遮挡不匹配、纹理投影伪影和视角相关外观引起的多视图不一致。

Method: 将混合渲染建模为多视图不一致问题，提出“消隐并补全”框架；利用由标量球谐高斯参数化的轻量级神经消隐场，学习网格消隐的位置及观察方向；通过连续透射率融合逐步显露网格后的残差高斯，保留重建梯度并避免硬可见性突变。

Result: 摘要报告，在结构化和半结构化场景中，该方法以更少的高斯基元实现更高视觉保真度和更快渲染；未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 允许对网格进行视角相关的选择性消隐，可突破纯加法残差补偿的局限，改善混合场景表示的质量与效率。

Abstract: Hybrid Mesh-Gaussian rendering combines compact textured surfaces with flexible 3D Gaussian primitives, offering an efficient alternative to purely Gaussian scene representations for structured environments. Existing scene-reconstruction hybrids commonly follow an additive formulation, where Gaussian residuals compensate for errors left by the mesh. However, not every Mesh-Gaussian conflict is caused by missing content: residual geometry errors, occlusion mismatch, texture-projection artifacts, and view-dependent appearance can make the same mesh content consistent in one view but inconsistent in another. We therefore formulate hybrid rendering as a multi-view inconsistency problem and present Gaussolve, a subtract-and-refill framework that selectively dissolves inconsistent mesh content before residual Gaussians refill the revealed regions. We introduce a lightweight Neural Dissolve Field, parameterized by scalar spherical-harmonic Gaussians, to learn where and from which viewing directions mesh content should be dissolved. A continuous transmittance fusion rule progressively exposes residual Gaussians behind the dissolved mesh, preserving their reconstruction gradients and avoiding hard visibility discontinuities. Experiments on structured and semi-structured scenes demonstrate higher visual fidelity with fewer Gaussian primitives and faster rendering. Project page: https://ooosn.github.io/gaussians-can-subtract/

</details>


### [5] [Expanding the Quality-Coverage Frontier with Competitive Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07055)
*Wenxuan Peng,Richard Zhang,Haitian Zheng,Jaskirat Singh,Tsai-Shien Chen,Eli Shechtman,Bharath Hariharan,Hadar Averbuch-Elor,Zongze Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过训练时的胜者通吃竞争和辅助条件缩放，改善流匹配生成模型的质量—覆盖率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一中间噪声图像可能对应多个干净图像，但常规流匹配预测单一速度，容易将不同可能性平均，限制生成质量与覆盖率。

Method: 为预测器加入随机低维条件编码z；对每个训练样本采样K个编码，生成K个候选速度，仅对最接近目标的候选计算损失。推理时使用辅助条件缩放，并与传统引导方法结合。

Result: 在ImageNet-256上，SiT使用不足原预训练计算量3%的后训练计算，在67% Recall时FD_{r,6}从19.22降至5.91；RAEv2在70% Recall时从16.83降至3.18。

Conclusion: 竞争式多候选训练结合两种引导机制，可显著改善质量—覆盖率前沿；摘要未给出完整实验细节或其他数据集结果。

Abstract: Flow matching (FM) generators often face a quality-coverage tradeoff. Inference-time techniques such as classifier-free guidance raise quality, but at the cost of coverage. We hypothesize this limitation stems from a basic property of the objective: an intermediate noisy image could correspond to many clean images, yet the model can only predict a single velocity, at best averaging over the multiple possibilities. To mitigate this, we introduce competition among predictions during training. We achieve this by augmenting the predictor with a randomly drawn low-dimensional conditioning code $z$. For each training example, we sample $K$ values of $z$ to produce $K$ candidate velocities that compete to explain the target, and apply the loss only to the best-matching candidate. This winner-take-all objective allows different modes to shape different predictions, rather than being averaged into a single compromise. Furthermore, this term naturally enables a new form of guidance, auxiliary conditioning scaling, which complements traditional guidance methods. Together, these two guidance mechanisms show that our method substantially improves the quality-coverage frontier. We validate on ImageNet-256, using two different testbeds, SiT and RAEv2. Given less than 3% of the original SiT pretraining compute for post-training, at the 67% Recall point, our method reduces $\mathrm{FD}_{r,6}$ from 19.22 to 5.91. On RAEv2, at the 70% Recall point, it reduces $\mathrm{FD}_{r,6}$ from 16.83 to 3.18.

</details>


### [6] [Segment What It Can Draw: Querying a Text-to-Image Generator with Its Own Images for Skin Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07061)
*Hongyu Cao,Guangfu Guo,Jinghan Zhang,Dakshak Keerthi Chandra,Ying Zhang,Kunpeng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GENIQ利用生成图像构建并细化皮肤与病灶原型，无需训练或推理时标注，即可实现皮肤病灶分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无掩膜标注的病灶分割需要其他目标提示；现有方法依赖皮肤镜专用规则、标注衍生先验或用户选区，而扩散模型的概念词注意力在皮肤镜图像上不可靠。

Method: 从生成的健康皮肤和病灶图像提取初始原型，再用无标签皮肤镜图像中最像皮肤和病灶的图像块细化原型；沿细化后的区分方向对测试图像块评分，并使用门控阈值抑制小病灶周围的上下文溢出。

Result: 在三个皮肤镜测试集上取得0.85–0.88的Dice分数；三个数据集上均优于零样本和基于词注意力的方法，并在两个数据集的Dice和IoU上优于手工设计的皮肤镜处理流程。

Conclusion: 生成图像提供的语义区分方向比词语派生方向更贴近真实皮肤与病灶的区分，可用于无需额外模型或推理时标注的训练自由分割；摘要未提供具体数据集名称或显著性检验。

Abstract: Skin lesion segmentation without annotated masks requires the target to be specified through other cues. Existing approaches rely on dermoscopy-specific rules, annotation-derived model priors, or user-selected regions. A general text-to-image diffusion transformer offers a text interface to the target, but its concept-word attention is unreliable on dermoscopy images: in the model's attention-output space, the word-derived skin-to-lesion direction is poorly aligned with the direction separating real skin from real lesions. We show that the direction derived from generated healthy-skin and lesion images is substantially better aligned. Based on this finding, we propose GENIQ, a training-free method that constructs initial skin and lesion prototypes from generated images and refines them using the most skin-like and lesion-like patches from unlabelled dermoscopy images. It scores test patches along the refined direction and applies a gated threshold to suppress contextual spill-over around small lesions. Without inference-time annotations or additional models, GENIQ achieves Dice scores of 0.85-0.88 on three dermoscopy test sets. It outperforms zero-shot and word-based attention methods on all three sets, and a hand-designed dermoscopy pipeline in Dice and IoU on two.

</details>


### [7] [SD-LoRA: Training-Time Structural Distillation into LoRA for Few-Shot Vision-Language Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07063)
*Mohammed Rahman Sherif Khan Mohammad,Ardhendu Behera,Sandip Pradhan,Swagat Kumar,Yonghuai Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SD-LoRA 在训练时利用图结构蒸馏增强 CLIP-LoRA 的少样本适应，推理时无需图计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量适应方法主要监督全局图像嵌入，未充分利用局部 token 结构；结构感知方法则往往增加推理开销。

Method: 训练阶段构建异构图教师，建模图像块间、全局与局部及视觉与文本关系；通过隐式蒸馏和原型预测对齐，将结构归纳偏置转移至共享的 CLIP-LoRA 骨干。推理时仅需一次前向传播和缓存式分类。

Result: 在 11 个少样本基准上，相比 CLIP-LoRA 基线，1、2、4、8、16-shot 设置分别提高 2.4、1.8、3.0、2.4、2.0 个百分点；其他迁移、分布外鲁棒性和校准实验也显示改善。

Conclusion: 训练时结构监督能够在不引入额外测试时图计算的情况下，提升少样本准确率和迁移可靠性。

Abstract: Few-shot adaptation of vision-language models must balance two competing goals: learning fine-grained visual distinctions from very limited supervision and preserving the efficiency that makes frozen CLIP-like models attractive for deployment. Existing lightweight adaptation methods, including prompt tuning, LoRA, and cache-based adapters, typically supervise global image embeddings and therefore underuse token-level structure. Conversely, structure-aware methods exploit local evidence but often introduce additional inference-time computation. We propose SD-LoRA, a structural distillation framework that uses token-level graph reasoning only during training and transfers its effects into a lightweight LoRA-adapted CLIP model. During training, a heterogeneous graph teacher models patch-patch, global-local, and visual-text relations over high-resolution ViT tokens. Rather than retaining this graph at test time, SD-LoRA transfers its structural bias into the shared CLIP-LoRA backbone through implicit distillation and Prototype Predictive Alignment, which aligns graph-conditioned representations with support-derived class prototypes. At inference, the graph teacher is discarded; prediction requires only a single CLIP-LoRA forward pass and a cache-based classifier. Across 11 few-shot benchmarks, SD-LoRA improves over a strong CLIP-LoRA baseline by 2.4/1.8/3.0/2.4/2.0 percentage points in the 1/2/4/8/16-shot settings, respectively, without additional graph computation at test time. Results on base-to-novel generalization, cross-dataset transfer, out-of-distribution robustness, and calibration further show that training-time structural supervision improves both accuracy and transfer reliability. Code is available at https://github.com/MR-Sherif/SD-LoRA.git.

</details>


### [8] [Wide View Synthesis from Overlapping Perspective Cameras in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07081)
*Kaixuan Zhang,Zhipeng Xiong,Minxian Li,Mingwu Ren,Xiatian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WV-GS利用前馈式3D高斯溅射，从多台透视相机合成几何一致的宽视角图像，无需逐场景优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像拼接难以处理相机中心分离导致的深度相关视差和遮挡；标准3DGS依赖逐场景优化，且宽视角合成还面临边缘拉伸和相机间光度不一致。

Method: 提出前馈式WV-GS；采用共享相机中心的局部透视投影进行分段透视渲染（PPR），缓解边缘拉伸；训练时使用空间仿射光度建模（SAPM）处理相机相关的光度差异。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明方法有效；提出含稠密宽视角真值的合成基准WideDrive，并为真实数据集采用基于LiDAR投影的稀疏参考评估协议。摘要未给出具体量化结果。

Conclusion: WV-GS为多相机宽视角合成提供了无需逐场景优化的方案，并针对几何、投影和光度问题进行了设计；数据集与代码计划公开。

Abstract: Multi-perspective-camera systems are widely adopted since they provide broad visual coverage for autonomous driving, motivating the synthesis of a unified wide view from spatially separated observations. In this work, we formulate wide view synthesis (WVS), which aims to synthesize a geometrically consistent wide view at a specified virtual viewpoint from multiple perspective cameras. Unlike conventional image stitching, WVS requires reasoning about 3D geometry and visibility to handle depth-dependent parallax and occlusions arising from spatially separated camera centers. While vanilla 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-fidelity view synthesis, its reliance on per-scene optimization limits its applicability to driving scenes. Furthermore, WVS introduces additional challenges, *e.g.*, peripheral stretching under perspective projection and photometric inconsistencies across cameras. To address these challenges, we propose **Wide-View Gaussian Splatting (WV-GS)**, a feed-forward Gaussian-splatting framework that synthesizes wide views without per-scene optimization. Specifically, we introduce Piecewise Perspective Rendering (PPR) to mitigate peripheral stretching through local perspective projections sharing a common camera center, and Spatial Affine Photometric Modeling (SAPM) to account for camera-dependent photometric variations during training. We further introduce **WideDrive**, a synthetic benchmark providing dense ground-truth wide views and adapt a LiDAR-projected sparse-reference protocol to real-world datasets. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of WV-GS for WVS. Dataset and code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [9] [A Proxy-Based Method for Measuring Racial Disparities in~Text-to-Image~Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07132)
*Nia Mucher,Ngan Pham,Jason Chin,Aaron Brown,David Evans*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: We present an automated method for auditing text-to-image generators for representational bias against ground-truth demographic benchmarks. Our approach adapts the racial proxy variables used in U.S. antidiscrimination law to provide a fine-grained comparative analysis of variations in racial representation in generated images. We classify each generated image by race and then compare the model's distribution to publicly available demographic benchmark data to identify patterns of over- and under-representation. We also measure skin tone using CIELAB values and calculate cosine similarity amongst groups of images in order to capture biases that don't show up in demographic comparisons such as differences in image quality across races and skin tones. We report on experiments testing three models (Imagen 4 Fast, Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large Turbo, and Flux Schnell) using three categories of proxies (geographic, occupational, and surname) and 50 variants within each category. We find notable variation across the models as well as by proxy category. For example, Imagen 4 Fast overrepresents white classified images more than the other models tested and produces images with lighter, more homogeneous skin tones. Imagen 4 Fast also had the largest representational disparity for Black demographics among surname and geographical prompts. Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large Turbo produces results that overall diverge most from census data but yield the most diverse range of skin tones. Our method yields a generalizable and legally-grounded auditing tool that surfaces detailed information about model outputs without relying on implicit normative definitions of fairness.

</details>


### [10] [Perceptual Distortion for Overfitted Video Coding: Sliced Matching in Space, Error Alignment in Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07148)
*Tianyi Yang,Ge Gao,Fan Zhang,David Bull*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结合局部切片Wasserstein失真和光流残差的视频编码框架，在固定码率下改善感知质量和时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐帧Wasserstein失真的独立高斯近似难以充分匹配特征分布；跨帧无约束的纹理重采样会导致时间不一致。

Method: 空间上使用局部切片Wasserstein失真，通过一维最优传输匹配经验特征分布，捕获通道间依赖并放宽精确空间对齐；时间上利用光流残差约束运动轨迹上的编码误差，避免额外施加重建保真度约束。

Result: 在UVG和HEVC Class B上使用过拟合编码器COOL-CHIC，相比逐帧WD，测试序列平均DISTS和LPIPS的BD-rate分别降低9.2%和3.0%；相同码率下tOF降低10.2%。

Conclusion: 区分可放宽的空间对应关系与需保持的时间对应关系，能够同时提升视频编码的感知效率和时间一致性。

Abstract: Recent works in neural video coding have increasingly been trading off perceptual quality against reconstruction fidelity at a fixed bitrate. Wasserstein Distortion (WD) offers a principled approach to balancing these objectives by relaxing spatial correspondences in feature matching, enabling lightweight overfitted image codecs to compete with computationally intensive generative decoders. Extending vanilla WD to video, however, presents two challenges: its independent Gaussian approximation limits the expressiveness of feature distribution matching, while unconstrained texture resampling across frames introduces temporal inconsistencies. We address these challenges by distinguishing between the correspondences that can be relaxed and those that must be preserved. Spatially, we introduce a local sliced Wasserstein distortion (SWD) that matches empirical feature distributions through one-dimensional optimal transport, capturing cross-channel dependencies without enforcing exact spatial alignment. Temporally, we employ a novel flow residual that constrains coding errors along motion trajectories, preserving temporal consistency without imposing additional reconstruction fidelity constraints. Evaluated on UVG and HEVC Class B with the overfitted codec COOL-CHIC, our framework achieves BD-rate reductions of 9.2% in DISTS and 3.0% in LPIPS relative to frame-wise WD, averaged over test sequences, while lowering tOF by 10.2% at matched rate.

</details>


### [11] [VINO: Revisiting 3D Representation Learning with Volume Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07176)
*Adrian Kruse,Kadir Yilmaz,Daan de Geus,Francis Engelmann,Bastian Leibe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VINO通过重设计3D骨干网络、自监督目标和2D到3D知识蒸馏，在无标签条件下学习可迁移的3D场景语义表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型已能学习通用图像表示，但3D场景表示学习尚未达到类似水平；现有方法的架构和训练目标存在局限。

Method: 采用类似ViT的Volume Transformer替代分层PTv3；在学生—教师框架中，让教师提供干净一致的目标、学生学习对强扰动的不变性；要求学生仅凭可见上下文预测被掩蔽区域的表示；使用视角一致的监督进行2D到3D知识蒸馏。

Result: 在室内和室外基准的密集线性探测中取得显著提升，并表现出较强的分布外迁移能力；摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 合理设计骨干网络、自监督任务和跨模态蒸馏，有助于提升无标签3D场景语义表示的质量和泛化能力。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning has given rise to vision foundation models that learn general-purpose image representations transferable across downstream tasks, yet representation learning for 3D scenes has not achieved comparable success. Motivated by this, we identify key limitations of current 3D representation learning approaches and revisit their core design choices. We first replace the commonly used hierarchical PTv3 backbone with the Volume Transformer, a ViT-like architecture for 3D scenes. We then redesign two core objectives of modern approaches built around student-teacher networks: matching representations across views and predicting masked content from visible context. For the first objective, we separate the roles of teacher and student: the teacher provides clean, consistent targets while the student learns invariance to stronger perturbations. For the second objective, we prevent the student from accessing the masked geometry and instead require it to infer missing representations from visible context only. Finally, we redesign 2D-to-3D knowledge distillation, replacing direct regression of view-dependent image features with view-consistent supervision. Together, these changes form VINO, a framework that learns semantic representations of 3D scenes without labels. VINO substantially improves dense linear probing across indoor and outdoor benchmarks and transfers strongly to out-of-domain settings.

</details>


### [12] [MammoSeek: A Mammography Agent with selective zooming for grounded reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07188)
*Haoyan Ding,Qian Wang,Lixia Wang,Debiao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MammoSeek通过模拟放射科医生的整体阅片与选择性放大流程，提高乳腺X线检查的评估与报告能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型通常以固定的低分辨率处理整幅图像，难以识别亚毫米级钙化，乳腺X线判读表现接近随机。

Method: 智能体先形成每侧乳房的整体印象，自主选择检测器候选区域，在全分辨率图像上放大检查；随后依据BI-RADS评估技能生成分级和带定位的术语结构化报告，并结合图像模型分数决定是否召回。

Result: 在内部EMBED和外部VinDr-Mammo数据集上，检查级AUC分别为0.824和0.818，优于所比较的多模态大语言模型、乳腺影像智能体和CNN分类器。选择性放大在定位、结构化报告F1及召回决策上优于不放大和强制放大，且成本较低。

Conclusion: 模拟临床阅片流程并按需检查高分辨率区域，有助于提升乳腺X线评估性能；摘要未提供置信区间或前瞻性临床验证结果。

Abstract: Radiologists read a screening mammogram by first forming a global impression of the four views and then selectively magnifying suspicious regions before assigning a BI-RADS assessment. MLLMs, in contrast, see the whole image at a fixed, reduced resolution, which makes sub-millimeter calcifications invisible, and they perform close to chance on mammography. Therefore, we present MammoSeek, a mammography agent that reproduces this reading process. After forming an impression of each breast, the agent performs selective zooming: it decides by itself which detector candidates deserve a closer look and inspects only those on full-resolution views. Guided by a BI-RADS assessment skill, it then assigns a category and generates a localized, lexicon-structured report for each breast; its assessment is combined with image-model scores in a decision layer to decide recall. On an internal (EMBED) and an external (VinDr-Mammo) dataset, \ours reaches exam-level AUCs of 0.824 and 0.818, far above other MLLMs, mammography agent and CNN classifiers. Selective zooming also outperforms both no zooming and forced zooming in localization, structured-report F1 and recall decisions at low cost.

</details>


### [13] [Householder Motion Embedding: Noise Modulation for Motion-Controllable Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07294)
*Tao Jun Lin,Jinhong Ni,Ruyi Zha,Yifu Wang,Xibin Song,Pan Ji,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HME 利用通道空间中的 Householder 反射将运动信号编码进噪声，在保持帧内高斯分布的同时引入跨帧相关性，实现高效的运动可控视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有噪声扭曲方法会破坏噪声的独立同分布高斯结构，需要雅可比等分布校正，计算开销较大。

Method: 依据用户提供的运动信号，构造一系列 Householder 反射，对各空间位置的通道向量施加正交变换；该方法与网络架构无关，可直接融入标准视频扩散模型微调流程。

Result: 在不同场景下，HME 的表现达到或超过现有最先进方法，速度较噪声扭曲方法提升 2.7 倍。摘要未提供具体数据集和评价指标。

Conclusion: 通道空间中的 Householder 正交变换为运动可控视频生成提供了一种有理论依据且高效的噪声扭曲替代方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models for video generation have achieved remarkable success, yet precise motion control under explicit user guidance remains challenging. Recent noise-warping methods introduce temporal correlations by warping the noise according to the user-provided motion signal. However, standard spatial warping generally disrupts the noise's i.i.d. Gaussian structure, requiring distribution correction, such as Jacobian-based correction, but at a substantial computational overhead. We propose Householder Motion Embedding (HME), which encodes the motion signal into the noise through a sequence of Householder reflections that together form an orthogonal transformation applied to each location's channel vector. HME exactly preserves the spatial Gaussian structure within each frame while introducing cross-frame correlations that promote temporally consistent video. HME is agnostic to the network architecture and integrates directly into standard video diffusion fine-tuning pipelines. Experiments on different scenarios show that HME matches or outperforms the state of the art, while achieving a 2.7x speedup over noise warping. These results demonstrate that channel-space Householder transformations offer a principled and efficient alternative to noise warping for motion-controllable video generation.

</details>


### [14] [ArtiVidRM: Verifiable Artifact-as-Rubric Reward Modeling for Explicit Quality Articulation in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07301)
*Caorui Li,Yangguang Ji,Shuxia Lin,Yu Chen,YiCheng Xiao,Yongliang Wu,Zhenxiang Jiang,Licheng Tang,Deng Li,Zhuxuanzi Wang,Jiahao Sun,Chonghan Liu,Lirian Su,Jay Wu,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArtiVidRM先识别视频生成伪影，再依据结构化伪影列表评分，并通过多奖励强化学习联合提升可解释性与偏好预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频奖励模型要么直接输出缺乏解释的标量分数，要么生成未被明确优化为最终判断依据的自由形式解释，难以提供可靠、可验证的优化信号。

Method: 采用“先观察、后评分”范式：识别可见生成伪影并形成结构化列表，以其作为质量评分依据；通过多奖励强化学习联合优化伪影预测和质量评估，其中伪影奖励衡量语义覆盖与预测效率，成对偏好目标通过分数差监督质量排序。

Result: 摘要报告该方法在视频偏好基准上达到最先进水平，能为视频扩散模型优化提供有效信号，并支持对闭源视频生成器进行伪影引导的改进；未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 联合优化结构化伪影表征与偏好预测，可使视频质量评分更有依据，并提升评估与生成优化效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become an effective approach for improving video generation, but its success depends heavily on reliable reward models. Existing methods either map videos directly to opaque scalar scores or generate free-form rationales that are not explicitly optimized as the basis of the final judgment. We propose ArtiVidRM, a verifiable, artifact-as-rubrics video reward model that follows an observe-then-score paradigm. It first identifies visible generation artifacts and organizes them into a structured artifact list, then uses these artifacts as explicit rubrics for quality assessment. This intermediate representation grounds each score in concrete video-level evidence, making the reward more interpretable and informative for optimization. We further introduce a multi-reward reinforcement learning strategy to jointly optimize artifact prediction and quality assessment. The reward decomposes into complementary objectives that evaluate artifact prediction quality, including semantic coverage and prediction efficiency, together with a pairwise preference objective that supervises quality ranking through score differences. By jointly optimizing the intermediate artifact representation and the downstream preference prediction, the model learns to produce informative artifact lists while improving the accuracy of video quality assessment. Experiments show that ArtiVidRM achieves state-of-the-art performance on video preference benchmarks, provides effective signals for video diffusion optimization, and enables artifact-guided refinement of closed-source video generators.

</details>


### [15] [Where Do Reported Long-Video QA Gains Come From? A Controlled Audit of Frame Selection, Evidence Injection, and Expert Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07329)
*Shih-Yao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Long-video question answering is usually studied as frame selection: which few frames should a vision-language model see? We introduce VideoRouteAudit, a controlled audit that separates this choice from two alternatives, injecting non-visual evidence and routing to another answering model. It holds the backbone, frame budget, decoding, prompt and grader fixed while varying one decision at a time. On the full Video-MME long bucket (n=900, K=8), no tested selector significantly improves over uniform sampling. The largest selection-only estimate is +1.9 pt (95% CI [-1.2,+5.0]) and our cascade is +1.2 pt [-1.7,+4.1]; all are equivalent to uniform within +/-5 pt. The same contrasts are unstable at the n=200 subset scale commonly used: three of four are up to 3.8x larger, one reverses sign, and resampled 200-item subsets reverse the full-bucket effect's sign in 35.8% of draws. The audit also corrects our own first results. The anomaly-domain "+ASR" arms injected no text, so the pooled +4.7 pt gain was selection plus a development prefix. With transcripts, ASR lowers XD-Violence accuracy by 10.0 pt when appended after the question and not at all when placed before it, and lowers UCF-Crime by 13.0 pt either way; on Video-MME, placing it first adds about 4 pt (7 on Qwen2-VL) to uniform sampling and to the cascade alike (post hoc, unadjusted). Swapping the answering model gives the largest contrast (+15.1 pt on MUSIC-AVQA, +33.3 pt on its worst type), but the same audio-visual expert with its soundtrack silenced keeps 92% of the gain (69% with a mismatched soundtrack), so the contrast is mostly a model swap. Code, per-item predictions and scripts that recompute every statistic are available at https://github.com/mikeslin-ir/long-video-qa-audit.

</details>


### [16] [MIKU: Material Invariance and Knowledge Unification for PBR Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07354)
*Yajie Bao,Jiaye Fu,Youtian Lin,Shuang Wu,Jiachen Qian,Feihu Zhang,Xun Cao,Yao Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIKU通过统一PBR材质表示和两阶段多视图扩散生成，提升3D材质的生成稳定性、物理合理性及语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集和合成流程缺乏统一标准；材质定义、渲染约定和参数化方式不一致，导致生成结果不稳定或不符合物理规律。

Method: 构建覆盖数据预处理至纹理合成的统一流程，并通过归一化协调不同材质参数。采用两阶段多视图生成：先在几何引导下生成跨视图一致的反照率图，再以反照率和统一表示为条件，利用物理感知扩散模型预测金属度与粗糙度图。

Result: 摘要报告，广泛实验显示该方法在纹理质量和物体部件间语义一致性方面达到最先进水平，并提高生成稳定性与物理一致性；未提供具体数据或对比基线。

Conclusion: 将表示标准化与外观合成解耦，有助于跨数据集学习和生成物理合理、语义一致的PBR材质，推动3D内容创建自动化。

Abstract: High-quality material generation for physically based rendering (PBR) remains challenging because datasets and synthesis pipelines lack standardized representations. Differences in material definitions, rendering conventions, and parameterizations lead to unstable and physically implausible results. We propose MIKU, a unified framework for standardized PBR asset representation and physically consistent material generation across diverse 3D content domains. Its core is a unified processing pipeline that standardizes the workflow from data preprocessing to final texture synthesis, maintaining consistent geometry and texture representations. A normalization scheme harmonizes heterogeneous material parameterizations in a common representation space, enabling robust cross-dataset training and inference. Building on this formulation, we train a novel physically aware diffusion model for PBR texture generation within a two-stage multi-view synthesis framework. First, geometry-aware albedo generation synthesizes multi-view albedo maps under explicit geometric guidance to ensure cross-view appearance consistency. Second, metallic-roughness generation uses the diffusion model to predict physically plausible roughness and metallic maps conditioned on the generated albedo and unified representation. Trained end-to-end on standardized data, the model learns to generate materials that respect category-level material priors and semantic part boundaries. By decoupling representation standardization from appearance synthesis, MIKU substantially improves material generation stability and physical consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art texture quality and semantic consistency across object parts, advancing automated material generation for 3D content creation.

</details>


### [17] [WeLM-Image: A Unified Image Generation Model towards Visual Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07392)
*Canmiao Fu,Chen Li,Guohui Zhang,Hao Lu,Hongyang Wei,Huadong Wu,Huaisong Zhang,Hubery Yin,Liyuan Cui,Qixin Yan,Tong Shao,Xiaoqiang Zhou,Xinhang Leng,Ying Zhang,Zhenhai Liu,Zhipeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WeLM-Image 将多模态理解、图像生成和编辑统一于单一模型，并通过稀疏架构与蒸馏支持单 GPU 部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模消费应用中，同时满足多功能图像生成、语义理解、编辑质量与单 GPU 推理效率的需求。

Method: 将文本、参考图像和目标潜变量组成交错序列；采用共享注意力、独立理解与生成参数的 MoT 架构，并在各路径引入 MoE。利用知识驱动视觉词汇、视觉聚类、双语标注及经验证的编辑监督构建数据，渐进训练至原生 1.5K 分辨率；分别优化生成和编辑策略，再以噪声感知的在策略蒸馏合并，并用 MeanFlow 进行少步蒸馏。

Result: 1536 分辨率模型在所评估的开源模型中，于 PRISM-Bench、Qwen-Image-Bench 和 UnicBench 的综合比较均排名第一。模型存储参数为 317 亿，每个目标潜变量 token 约激活 54 亿生成参数；Turbo 版本在单张 NVIDIA H20 上以 8 步无引导推理生成 1024×1024 图像，耗时 4.98 秒。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，统一多模态架构、针对性数据和策略蒸馏可结合高质量图像生成与编辑，并实现较高效的单 GPU 推理；摘要未提供各基准具体分数及消融结果。

Abstract: We present \textbf{WeLM-Image}, a unified image foundation model that integrates multimodal understanding into text-to-image generation, instruction-guided editing, and multi-reference synthesis. Designed for single-GPU deployment in WeChat's large-scale consumer applications, the model processes text, reference images, and target latents as one interleaved sequence. A Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) backbone combines shared attention with independent understanding and generation parameters to limit optimization interference. Equipping each path with Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) modules provides sparse capacity, with 31.7B stored parameters and approximately 5.4B generation parameters activated per target latent token. A generation-oriented data engine combines a knowledge-grounded visual vocabulary with large-scale visual clustering to balance semantic coverage and editing tasks, while training-time evaluation guides iterative data refinement. Bilingual annotations and verified editing supervision support progressive training up to native 1.5K resolution. We optimize generation and editing separately with rubric- and checklist-based rewards, then consolidate the specialized policies through noise-aware on-policy distillation. In the reported overall comparisons, the 1536-resolution model ranks first among the evaluated open-source models on PRISM-Bench, Qwen-Image-Bench, and UnicBench. MeanFlow-based few-step distillation yields WeLM-Image-Turbo, which generates a $1024\times1024$ image in 4.98\,s on a single NVIDIA H20 GPU using eight guidance-free steps.

</details>


### [18] [Vis2State: Visual Reasoning through Executable State Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07397)
*Sheng Jin,Wentao Liu,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Vis2State 通过显式、可修订的问题状态连接视觉检查与程序求解，在四项基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将视觉假设和任务约束直接嵌入求解代码，会使问题表示难以独立检查和修订。

Method: 提出无需训练的智能体框架：模型无需预定义谜题模式或求解程序，即可构建问题状态并生成求解代码；利用视觉检查和执行反馈，分别修订问题表示与求解程序。

Result: 在四项基准上达到最先进性能；跨模型评估显示一致的性能提升。消融实验、错误分析及固定程序下的状态干预支持该设计的有效性。摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 以状态为中心的设计有助于将视觉理解与程序求解解耦，从而改善视觉谜题求解；代码计划公开。

Abstract: Solving visual puzzles requires both accurate interpretation of visual evidence and reliable computation under task-specific rules. Embedding visual assumptions and task constraints directly in solving code makes the represented problem difficult to inspect and revise independently. We introduce Vis2State, a training-free agentic framework that abstracts visual puzzles into explicit, revisable problem states linking visual inspection and execution. Visual inspection guides state construction and refinement, while generated programs solve the represented problem. The model constructs both the state and the program without predefined puzzle schemas or solving procedures. Inspection and execution feedback allow it to revise the problem representation and solving procedure independently. Vis2State achieves the state-of-the-art performance on four benchmarks. Cross-model evaluation further demonstrates consistent gains. Ablations, error analyses, and fixed-program state interventions further support the effectiveness of the state-centered design. The code will be made publicly available to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [19] [FreePath: Neural Mean Free Path for Image Dehazing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07398)
*Rongrong Gao,Jingxuan Feng,Wenyu Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreePath 将单幅图像去雾建模为特征空间中的路径状态恢复，在保留雾形成过程物理启发的同时避免直接估计大气参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式反演散射变量在复杂场景中可能不稳定；直接学习图像恢复映射则往往忽视雾形成的物理结构。

Method: 采用残差编码器—解码器。平均自由程残差块学习路径场和碰撞场，以引导雾约束校正；状态守恒集成模块在深层特征聚合时保留已恢复的路径状态。

Result: 在成对 CDD-11 雾图和真实 RTTS 场景上的实验中，相比代表性去雾基线，全参考保真度、无参考感知质量和视觉真实性均有提升；摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 以平均自由程为物理启发的特征状态恢复是单幅图像去雾的一种有效方案，但其提升幅度及泛化边界仍需结合完整实验评估。

Abstract: Single image dehazing requires recovering scene radiance from visual responses that are weakened and veiled by participating media. Existing methods either invert explicit scattering variables, which can be unstable under complex scenes, or learn direct restoration mappings, which often discard the physical organization of haze formation. We present \emph{FreePath}, a neural mean free path framework for image dehazing. Instead of estimating atmospheric parameters, FreePath represents dehazing as feature space path state restoration, inspired by the expected distance a visual response can propagate before interacting with haze. Within a residual encoder and decoder, the Mean Free Path Residual Block learns path and collision fields to guide haze constrained correction, while FreePath State Conservation Integration preserves restored path states during deep feature aggregation. Experiments on paired CDD-11 haze images and real RTTS scenes show that FreePath consistently improves full reference fidelity, no reference perceptual quality, and visual faithfulness over representative dehazing baselines.

</details>


### [20] [PSGAgent: Enhancing MLLMs' Dynamic Visual Perception for Panoptic Scene Graph Generation via Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07408)
*Qingyun Liu,Yuankun Liu,Jiaye Feng,Jie Huang,Zonghong Dai,Haochen Li,Jingkun Wang,Weiping Li,Tong Mo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PSGAgent通过动态视觉工具和两阶段训练，提升全景场景图生成中细粒度关系的识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型受固定分辨率视图限制，容易遗漏小物体或边缘物体的关系，也难以区分复杂场景中的歧义谓词。

Method: 构建“思考—行动—观察”智能体框架，利用区域放大工具探索局部关系、视觉高亮工具分割关系相关区域并抑制背景干扰；先用错误感知的多模态思维链数据进行冷启动训练，再结合分层奖励与组序列策略优化（GSPO）学习自适应工具使用。

Result: 在PSG基准上的实验达到当时最先进水平（SOTA）；摘要未提供具体指标或数值。

Conclusion: 动态视觉感知与经过优化的工具使用策略有助于兼顾场景的整体理解和细粒度关系识别。

Abstract: Panoptic Scene Graph generation (PSG) requires holistic yet fine-grained reasoning over complex relations among objects. However, current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) applied to PSG suffer from a perception bottleneck: they function as static observers limited by fixed-resolution views. As a result, models often overlook critical relations involving small or peripheral objects and struggle to differentiate ambiguous predicates in complex scenes. To address these limitations, we propose PSGAgent, an agentic framework that enhances visual perception for PSG through a think-act-observe loop. PSGAgent allows MLLMs to dynamically refine their visual perception using two tools: a Region Zoom-In Tool for local exploration of small or boundary relations and a Visual Highlight Tool for relation-focused segmentation that filters out irrelevant contextual noise. To optimize decision-making, we construct a two-stage training pipeline. First, we create a high-quality, error-aware Multimodal Chain-of-Thought (M-CoT) dataset that provides robust supervision for cold-start learning. Then, we design a novel hierarchical reward mechanism integrated with Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO) to enable strategic and adaptive tool usage. Extensive experiments on the PSG benchmark show that PSGAgent achieves SOTA performance, demonstrating strong capability in both holistic and fine-grained scene understanding.

</details>


### [21] [TRiST: Topology-Reinforced Swin Transformer for Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07431)
*Pengfei Gu,Huimin Li,Guangyu Meng,Hao Zheng,Haoteng Tang,Bin Fu,Danny Z. Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRiST 将多尺度持久同调融入 Swin Transformer 的特征表示、细化与注意力，在医学图像分类和分割任务中取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像中的连通分量和环等拓扑结构跨越多个尺度，而现有层次化视觉 Transformer 未在多尺度表示中显式建模拓扑信息。

Method: 在 Swin 四个原生阶段分别计算二维 H0/H1 持久同调并对齐 token 网格；通过门控残差网络分别细化短、中、长持久性信息；将阶段特定描述符转换为键显著性分数，作为偏置直接调整移位窗口注意力 logits。该框架分别实例化为分类模型 Topo-SwinV2-B 和分割模型 Topo-Swin-UNet。

Result: 在五个医学图像数据集上，相比 Swin 基线取得一致提升，与已有拓扑增强方法相比具有竞争力；摘要未提供具体指标或提升幅度。

Conclusion: 在多个原生尺度显式融合拓扑信息，有望提升基于 Swin 的医学图像分类与分割性能；具体效果和泛化能力仍需结合完整实验数据评估。

Abstract: Medical images contain topological patterns, such as connected components and loops, at scales ranging from small lesions to organ boundaries. Although hierarchical Vision Transformers have advanced medical image analysis, they do not explicitly incorporate topology throughout their multiscale representations. We propose Topology-Reinforced Swin Transformer (TRiST), a framework that integrates topological information into feature construction, refinement, and attention. First, a Hierarchical Multi-Scale Topology Representation algorithm computes 2D persistent homology (H0 and H1) independently at four native Swin stages, producing descriptors aligned with each stage's token grid rather than downsampling fine-scale topology. Second, a Band-Aware H0/H1 Residual Refinement module uses gated residual networks to process short-, medium-, and long-persistence information separately. Third, a Persistence-Band Progressive Attention Bias converts stage-specific descriptors into learned key-saliency scores that directly modify shifted-window attention logits. We instantiate TRiST as Topo-SwinV2-B for classification and Topo-Swin-UNet for segmentation, preserving the underlying Swin-based backbones. Experiments on five medical image datasets show consistent improvements over Swin-based baselines and competitive performance relative to existing topology-augmented methods.

</details>


### [22] [VIPA: View-Invariant Projector-Based Adversarial Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07443)
*Jiyu Han,Qingyue Deng,Bingyao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VIPA通过多视角仿真与联合优化生成投影对抗图案，提升物理攻击的视角鲁棒性、跨分类器适用性和隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐蔽投影攻击通常以单一静态视角建模投影与拍摄过程，难以适应视角变化和动态场景。

Method: 采用视角不变的投影仪—相机系统仿真，模拟投影图案与真实表面的物理交互；聚合多个视角的模拟攻击损失，联合优化对抗投影，再将图案投射到真实场景中。

Result: 摘要报告，在定向和非定向攻击实验中，VIPA相较现有方法取得更高的攻击成功率，同时提高隐蔽性，并在所有测试视角下保持较低感知退化；未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 多视角仿真和联合优化有助于实现对视角变化更鲁棒、可攻击不同分类器的物理投影攻击，但摘要未说明具体实验规模及泛化边界。

Abstract: Projector-based adversarial attack aims to physically manipulate real-world scenes by projecting adversarial patterns, thereby causing deep image classifiers to produce incorrect predictions. However, existing stealthy projector-based adversarial attack methods model the project-and-capture process in a single and static view, limiting their view-invariant capability and practical applicability in dynamic environments. In this paper, we introduce View-Invariant Projector-Based Adversarial Attack (VIPA), a novel method designed to overcome these limitations by achieving both view-invariant and classifier-agnostic adversarial attacks in the physical world. We first leverage a view-invariant projector-camera system simulation method to model the physical interactions between projected patterns and real-world surfaces. To ensure robustness and stealthiness of the attack across different views, VIPA optimizes adversarial projections by aggregating simulated attack losses from multiple views. This joint optimization across diverse views helps maintain robustness regardless of the viewpoints. Finally, the optimized patterns are physically projected into real-world scenes, where they successfully fool various classifiers from different viewpoints, thereby enabling robust and practical view-invariant adversarial attacks. Our experiments in both targeted and untargeted attacks demonstrate that VIPA consistently achieves higher attack success rates than existing methods, while also enhancing stealthiness and ensuring minimal perceptual degradation across all tested views.

</details>


### [23] [Compact Motion Embeddings for Feed-forward Unified Human Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07481)
*Seonwook Park,Hyunsoo Cha,Amrita Mazumdar,Shengze Wang,Tianye Li,Hanbyul Joo,Koki Nagano,Shalini De Mello*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FUHA通过学习与外观解耦的全身紧凑运动表征，实现无需外部姿态估计器的实时参考图像动画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有身体动画方法依赖外部姿态估计器和多步去噪，而紧凑的学习式运动表征大多仅覆盖面部，难以同时兼顾全身表达、外观解耦和速度。

Method: 使用Q-Former将冻结视觉编码器提取的驱动图像特征压缩为运动嵌入；通过对比损失拉近该嵌入与驱动者关键点渲染图的嵌入，并推远同一人在其他姿态下的嵌入。前馈Transformer基于参考图像和运动嵌入生成动画；推理时无需外部姿态估计器、关键点或参数化人体模型。

Result: 运动嵌入作为检索键时，身体姿态匹配优于10种视觉编码器的全局嵌入；同人检索率为0.1%，DINOv3骨干为9.5%。在上半身基准上，所有自重演指标及跨人重演身份相似度均优于基线；面部和全身任务表现具有竞争力。单张RTX 4090上，512×512分辨率最高达到38.9 FPS。

Conclusion: FUHA展示了紧凑、较少携带身份外观信息的全身运动嵌入，可支持高质量且实时可行的人体动画；摘要未说明各项指标的具体数值或跨数据集泛化情况。

Abstract: Animating a reference image with a driver's motion requires a motion representation that captures face, hand, and body articulation but excludes the driver's appearance. Most body animation methods take motion from external pose estimators and render it with many denoising steps; compact learned motion representations mostly cover the face. We present FUHA, a real-time-capable feed-forward transformer that learns a compact motion embedding of the whole human and uses it for face, upper-body, and full-body animation. A Q-Former compresses the driver's features from a frozen visual encoder into this embedding. To disentangle motion from appearance, a novel contrastive loss pulls it toward the motion embedding of the driver's rendered keypoint image and away from that of the same person in another pose. At inference time, FUHA needs no external pose estimators, keypoints, or parametric body models. As a retrieval key, the motion embedding matches body pose better than the learned global embeddings of ten vision encoders. Its same-person retrieval rate is 0.1%, against 9.5% for its own DINOv3 backbone. On our upper-body benchmark, FUHA outperforms all baselines on every self-reenactment metric and on cross-reenactment identity similarity. It is competitive on face and full-body and runs at up to 38.9 FPS at 512x512 on a single RTX 4090.

</details>


### [24] [Degree-Equalized View-Graph Selection under Tight Pair Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07498)
*Minhyeok Im,Jongseong Brad Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DEAL 通过在固定图像对预算下均衡图像连接度，无需训练即可提升 SfM 图像注册率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全图像对匹配成本随图像数量呈二次增长；现有一次性选择方法忽视已分配的节点度数，导致边集中、部分图像连接不足，即使图接近连通也可能重建失败。

Method: 提出免训练的贪心算法 Degree-Equalized Allocation（DEAL），根据候选图像对两端已有的连接覆盖程度，以指数方式降低已充分覆盖端点相关图像对的权重。

Result: 在九个大型 1DSfM 集合、预算 B=2N 下，同一先验的一次性选择器平均注册图像比例为 0.776，DEAL 为 0.929；SfM 监督的边先验为 0.930。在 38 场景标准基准上，DEAL 平均值为 0.875，高于一次性选择的 0.858。

Conclusion: 在有限匹配预算下，均衡分配图像连接可改善重建覆盖，且无需训练即可接近监督方法的表现；摘要未提供计算开销或统计显著性信息。

Abstract: Fully matching all image pairs in Structure-from-Motion is quadratic in the number of images, so practical pipelines pass a budgeted subset to the final matching and reconstruction backend. One-shot selectors score candidates with image retrieval, cheap geometric probes, or a learned prior and finalize B pairs before full matching. Their priorities do not account for the degree already allocated to each image, which can concentrate edges on a few images. Across nine large 1DSfM internet photo collections at B=2N, the coverage top-B selector leaves 54% of covered images with realized degree at most two. Reconstruction can then fail even when the selected graph is nearly connected and almost fully verified. Our training-free greedy rule, Degree-Equalized Allocation (DEAL), exponentially downweights pairs whose endpoints are already well covered. On these collections, DEAL improves the one-shot selector built on the same prior (from 0.776 to 0.929) and matches the mean registered-image ratio of an SfM-supervised edge prior under the shared backend (0.929 vs 0.930), without training. On the largely saturated 38-scene standard benchmark suite, DEAL gives the highest mean among the evaluated training-free selectors (0.875 vs 0.858 one-shot).

</details>


### [25] [Coverage Hides Churn: Auditing and Preserving Conformal Decisions as VLM Label Registries Grow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07502)
*Jai Kumar Sharma,Peeyush Tapadiya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 标签列表扩展会显著改变视觉语言分类器对旧标签的保形预测决策，即使覆盖率基本不变；PrefixCP通过固定各类别的历史标签参照集合保持旧决策稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示动态标签注册表中，softmax归一化随新增标签变化导致旧标签决策漂移，而传统覆盖率指标难以发现这一问题。

Method: 提出生命周期审计，同时衡量决策变化、覆盖率和预测集合大小；提出PrefixCP，为每个类别保留其加入时的标签注册表，并通过一次前缀扫描计算历史归一化项。

Result: 重新校准后，11种设置中53%—90%的新图像至少有一个旧标签决策发生变化；5种控制设置中覆盖率变化不超过0.01，但60%—67%的图像决策发生变化。PrefixCP缩小了固定参照与重新校准方案之间40.9%—58.5%的集合大小差距。

Conclusion: 在可交换性假设下，PrefixCP保持旧决策不变及精确的类别条件覆盖，同时在预测集合大小方面取得折中；覆盖率之外应审计决策稳定性。

Abstract: Vision-language classifiers can learn new labels from text alone, so the label lists they serve keep growing. We show that this growth silently changes conformal decisions about old labels. Common softmax-based conformal scores compare each label with an average over all labels; adding labels changes that average, and with it the score of every old label, even when the model, the image, and the old calibration data stay the same. Recalibrating after the update keeps the coverage guarantee, yet across eleven settings it changes at least one old decision on 53-90% of new images. Coverage cannot reveal this, because it checks only the true label: in five controlled settings that keep the old calibration, so that only the normalizer changes, old-class coverage moves by at most 0.01 while 60-67% of images change. We propose a lifecycle audit that measures these changes alongside coverage and set size, and a simple fix, PrefixCP: each class keeps the label registry that existed when it was added. Its old decisions then never change, each class keeps exact class-conditional coverage under exchangeability, and one prefix scan computes all historical normalizers at once. PrefixCP recovers 40.9-58.5% of the set-size gap between never updating the reference and recalibrating, which gives smaller sets but changes old decisions.

</details>


### [26] [Concertina: Embodying Manipulation and Cognition in One Diffusion Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07513)
*Chengcheng Wang,Wei He,Wenhao Li,Hongguang Li,Kai Han,Jianyuan Guo,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Concertina 将连续动作轨迹直接融入共享扩散式视觉语言模型，以极少额外参数实现机器人操作与认知的一体化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言动作策略将动作生成与视觉语言认知分离：离散动作令牌的误差难以稳定对应物理位移，而独立动作专家的梯度可能与主干更新竞争或无法传回主干。

Method: 提出具身视觉语言模型 Concertina：将每个控制时刻编码为主干隐藏空间中的连续动作令牌，使用共享 Transformer 的污染—重建机制按块生成时序轨迹，并直接施加物理坐标监督；通过 Action Interface Audit 分析跨时间轨迹耦合。

Result: 4.46B 参数模型中，活跃动作投影占比仅 0.21%，无需独立动作生成层；审计显示跨时间耦合主要出现在共享主干的中后层。LIBERO 平均成功率为 98.15%，LIBERO-Plus 为 85.9%，CALVIN ABC-D 平均完成链长度为 4.142。

Conclusion: 共享视觉语言主干能够同时承担认知和连续动作生成，减少对独立动作专家的依赖，并在多个基准上取得所报告的表现；摘要未提供完整对比或统计显著性信息。

Abstract: Most vision-language-action (VLA) policies decouple manipulation from cognition by bolting an external action mechanism onto a vision-language model (VLM)--either quantizing actions into discrete tokens whose errors bear no stable correspondence to physical displacements, or delegating continuous trajectory generation to a separate action expert whose gradients compete with or are blocked from updating the VLM backbone. We argue that manipulation and cognition should instead be embodied in the same model, shifting from a decoupled VLA to an Embodied Vision-Language Model (eVLM). We introduce Concertina, an eVLM that places continuous trajectory representation, blockwise generation, and physical-coordinate supervision directly on the computation and parameter path of a shared diffusion VLM backbone. Each control timestep is encoded as one continuous latent action token in the backbone hidden space, and the shared Transformer reconstructs temporally ordered action blocks via the same corrupt-and-reconstruct operator used for multimodal pretraining. Active action projections account for only 0.21% of parameters in the 4.46B model with zero separate action-generator layers, compared with 16.5%-35.1% dedicated action-expert capacity in representative continuous VLAs. Using our proposed Action Interface Audit, we confirm that cross-time trajectory coupling emerges in the middle and late layers of the shared backbone rather than at the action interfaces. On standard benchmarks, Concertina achieves 98.15% average success on regular LIBERO, 85.9% on the 10,030-task LIBERO-Plus benchmark, and 4.142 average completed chain length on CALVIN ABC-D.
  Code: https://github.com/lose4578/Concertina Hugging Face: https://huggingface.co/collections/Traly/concertina

</details>


### [27] [Action-Conditioned VLM Monitoring for Autonomous Vehicle Semantic Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07540)
*Daniel Adejumo,Omkar Ankalkope,Kunal Runwal,Aliasghar Arab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出动作条件化的自动驾驶异常监测方法，在生成视频数据上优于单阶段视频基线，但特异度较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义异常的驾驶场景不必然意味着车辆行为异常；同一场景下，监测标签取决于自车实际动作是否恰当。

Method: 使用视觉语言模型从隐藏结果的视频窗口预测场景所需动作，再依据明确的兼容性规则，将预测动作与实际自车运动轨迹比较；构建涵盖112组配对场景的六字段分类体系及带估计轨迹的生成视频语料。

Result: 在15类场景的235段视频上，固定T-2.5秒窗口的两阶段方法准确率为83.0%，异常召回率为0.773，优于单阶段视频基线的74.5%和0.536；特异度为0.880，低于基线的0.928。

Conclusion: 动作条件化的两阶段监测可提高异常检出能力，但存在特异度下降的权衡；结果来自生成视频语料上的探索性实验，真实场景泛化能力尚待验证。

Abstract: We study action-conditioned monitoring of autonomous-vehicle behavior in semantically unusual driving scenes. The target is whether the executed ego action is appropriate for the scene: the same scene can admit both normal and anomalous behavior, so visual unusualness alone does not determine the monitoring label. A Vision-Language Model (VLM) first predicts the scene-required action from an outcome-hidden video window; the monitor then compares that expectation with the realized ego-motion trajectory under an explicit compatibility rule. To study this monitoring task, we contribute a six-field taxonomy of 112 paired scenarios spanning both failure polarities (42 derived from documented incidents), a generated video corpus with estimated trajectories, and an exploratory two-stage monitor on that corpus. On 235 clips from 15 scenario families, the two-stage protocol with a fixed T-2.5 s window obtains 83.0% accuracy and anomaly recall 0.773, versus 74.5% and 0.536 for a single-stage video-only baseline; specificity is 0.880 versus 0.928.

</details>


### [28] [OptIMerge: Optimization-Based Merging of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07543)
*Viraj Shah,Ashwini Pokle,Peyman Milanfar,Mauricio Delbracio,Hossein Talebi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OptIMerge 通过优化少量、架构感知的合并参数，缓解图像到图像扩散模型合并时的参数干扰，实现接近联合训练的多任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型汤及逐元素合并方法难以处理图像修复专家模型中相互冲突的空间先验，容易产生明显伪影；联合训练则计算成本较高。

Method: 冻结所有专家模型，使用 100–500 张图像最小化多任务扩散损失，学习轻量合并参数。注意力层采用带显式通道偏置的结构化低秩特征对齐；卷积层采用逐卷积核合并系数及低秩 Cayley 变换，通过正交旋转对齐通道。可训练合并参数相当于网络参数规模的不足 0.4%。

Result: 摘要称，该方法同时支持多个任务，性能可媲美计算成本较高的联合训练，并明显优于零样本模型汤；摘要未提供具体指标或逐任务结果。

Conclusion: 架构感知的优化式合并可有效缓解 I2I 扩散专家之间的表示冲突，以较小的参数和数据开销获得较强的多任务能力；其泛化范围仍需结合完整实验验证。

Abstract: The arithmetic merging of weights from multiple task-specific models fine-tuned from a shared base model, commonly known as model souping, has emerged as a popular strategy for multi-task learning without the computational overhead of joint retraining. However, naive scalar weight averaging frequently leads to severe performance degradation when the constituent models learn structurally divergent feature representations, leading to destructive parameter interference. We study this problem in a largely unexplored setting: merging image-to-image (I2I) diffusion models fine-tuned for dense, pixel-accurate tasks such as image restoration. Unlike the large language models (LLMs) and text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models for which most merging methods were developed, I2I experts encode conflicting spatial priors (e.g., anisotropic motion-blur vs. isotropic defocus-blur) in their convolutional filters, where both naive soup and LLM-style element-wise merging introduce visible artifacts. We introduce OptIMerge, a gradient-based optimization framework: instead of relying on heuristic scalars, OptIMerge learns highly expressive, yet parameter-efficient merger coefficients by minimizing a multi-task diffusion loss over a small set of 100-500 images. OptIMerge parameterizes the merge with architecture-aware transformations: for attention layers, a structured low-rank feature alignment with explicit channel biases; for convolutional layers, per-kernel merger coefficients, and a low-rank Cayley transform that aligns channels by orthogonal rotation without distorting the filters. By keeping the experts frozen and optimizing only these lightweight merger parameters (equivalent to <0.4% of the network footprint), OptIMerge achieves simultaneous multi-task capabilities that rival computationally expensive joint training and decisively outperform zero-shot model soups.

</details>


### [29] [Training-Free Predictive Spatio-Temporal Token Pruning for Few-Step Autoregressive Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07551)
*Jia Li,Xurui Peng,Xing Wang,Hayden Kwok-Hay So*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PreSTP 利用时空 token 冗余，以无需训练的剪枝方式加速少步自回归视频扩散，并兼顾生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步蒸馏使跨去噪步骤的冗余减少，现有免训练缓存方法难以进一步加速，更难适用于单步生成。

Method: 利用模型自身去噪轨迹中的早期干净估计，检测相邻帧静态内容的跨帧冗余和提前收敛 token 的跨步冗余；构建统一 token 级掩码，跳过冗余计算并恢复最近一次计算值，同时自适应复用掩码以降低密集探测成本。

Result: 5 秒视频生成加速 1.52–3.20 倍，效率与保真度权衡优于 TeaCache、FlowCache 等基线；30 秒生成最高加速 1.96 倍，同时提升 VBench-Long 表现并抑制长期漂移；单步生成中仍可降低推理成本。

Conclusion: 基于早期干净估计的时空 token 剪枝能够在无需额外训练或参考视频的情况下有效加速视频生成，即使不存在跨步冗余也适用。

Abstract: Few-step autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models generate videos chunk by chunk and have made efficient video generation practical. However, further accelerating these models toward real-time generation remains challenging, as few-step distillation leaves little cross-step redundancy for existing training-free caching methods to exploit. We revisit this regime and identify two token-level redundancy patterns: cross-frame redundancy from static content shared across adjacent frames, and cross-step redundancy from tokens that converge early during denoising. Crucially, we find that early clean estimates along the model's own denoising trajectory provide an effective online signal for locating both patterns, requiring no retraining, auxiliary predictor, or reference video. Based on these findings, we propose PreSTP (Predictive Spatio-Temporal Token Pruning), a training-free framework that constructs a unified token-wise redundancy mask from cross-frame similarity and cross-step convergence. Tokens marked as redundant skip subsequent computation under a consistent last-computed-value restoration rule, and a content-adaptive mask reuse strategy further reduces dense probing cost. On 5-second generation, PreSTP achieves 1.52x-3.20x speedups and a better efficiency-fidelity trade-off than coarse-grained caching baselines such as TeaCache and FlowCache. On 30-second generation, it reaches up to 1.96x speedup while improving VBench-Long and suppressing long-horizon drift. Notably, it continues to reduce inference cost even in the extreme one-step regime, where cross-step redundancy vanishes entirely and coarse step-wise caching is no longer applicable.

</details>


### [30] [Improving Long-Tail Detection via Layout-Instruction Conditioned Diffusion Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07553)
*Xinyu Zhang,Xiaodi Wang,Yanpeng Sun,Lingfeng Yang,Jingdong Wang,Lingqiao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tail-SD通过文本、布局及其混合条件生成合成数据，改善长尾目标检测，尤其提升稀有类别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有类别样本不足，且现有扩散生成方法通常仅支持文本或布局等单一条件，限制了长尾检测性能。

Method: 提出基于Stable Diffusion的布局—指令条件生成框架，支持结合边界框、掩码和文本提示进行可控合成；利用多任务交叉注意力模块与多任务嵌入令牌，使单一模型能够处理异构条件。

Result: 在COCO和LVIS上的实验表明，混合条件合成数据持续改善检测性能；在LVIS验证集上，稀有类别的边界框AP和掩码AP分别提升4.89%和5.68%。

Conclusion: 混合条件扩散合成有助于增强长尾目标覆盖和检测性能，为缓解稀有类别数据不足提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Synthetic data from diffusion models can improve object detection, but long-tail performance is still limited by scarce rare classes and inflexible conditioning (e.g., text-only or layout-only inputs). In this paper, we propose Tail-SD, a layout-instruction conditioned stable diffusion framework that supports text, layouts, or their hybrid, enabling controllable synthesis from boxes, masks, and/or prompts to better cover the long-tail objects. To train a single model across heterogeneous conditions, we introduce a multi-task cross-attention module and multi-task embedding tokens, improving generation quality and diversity. Extensive experiments on COCO and LVIS demonstrate that diffusion synthesis with hybrid conditioning consistently benefits detection, e.g., on LVIS val, it achieves +4.89% / +5.68% rare Box/Mask AP, highlighting strong potential for addressing long-tail challenges.

</details>


### [31] [MetricTrail: Revisiting the Power of Metric Depth and Local Optimization for Long-Sequence Streaming 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07555)
*Jingnan Gao,Jiahui Huang,Sven Elflein,Jianhao Zheng,Bingxin Ke,Xiaokang Yang,Yichao Yan,Laura Leal-Taixé,Qunjie Zhou,Jiawei Ren,Shengyu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MetricTrail 利用单目度量深度和 DINOv3 特征增强稀疏视觉里程计，实现长视频流式相机运动估计与稠密重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长单目视频的轨迹估计与稠密重建需要兼顾精度、推理效率和资源开销；作者探索在不扩大优化窗口或额外进行长序列训练的情况下提升性能。

Method: 基于 DPVO 稀疏局部估计器，用单图度量深度初始化图像块深度，并在优化中作为软约束；通过可学习适配器将冻结的 DINOv3 表征融入匹配特征和循环上下文。对应估计器及适配器在多样短视频上训练；模块化设计支持独立升级深度模型，以及无需重新训练地使用已知相机内参和标定双目观测。

Result: 在所报告的仅 RGB 协议下，MetricTrail 在 KITTI、Oxford Spires 和 VBR 上取得参评方法中最低的平均轨迹误差；结合单图深度预测，在室内外基准和真实场景视频上实现良好稠密重建。使用给定内参时，KITTI-02 的处理速度为 12.70 FPS，GPU 峰值内存为 3.21 GB。

Conclusion: 单图度量深度约束与预训练视觉表征可有效增强稀疏局部估计，为长视频运动估计和重建提供高效、可扩展的模块化方案；具体优势受摘要所述评测协议和参评方法范围限制。

Abstract: We present MetricTrail, a streaming system for accurate camera motion estimation and dense reconstruction from long monocular videos. To keep inference efficient over long sequences, we revisit DPVO's sparse local estimator. We find that single-image metric depth substantially improves its trajectory accuracy, without retraining the correspondence network or enlarging the optimization window. Metric predictions initialize patch depths and remain as soft constraints during optimization. We further improve correspondence estimation through learned adapters that augment matching features and recurrent context with frozen DINOv3 representations. The correspondence estimator and adapters are trained on diverse short clips without additional long-sequence training. This modular design also allows the depth provider to be independently upgraded and supports supplied camera intrinsics and calibrated stereo observations without retraining. Under the reported RGB-only protocols, MetricTrail achieves the lowest average trajectory error among evaluated methods on KITTI, Oxford Spires, and VBR. Pairing the estimated poses with single-image depth predictions also produces strong dense reconstruction on indoor and outdoor benchmarks and coherent reconstructions of in-the-wild videos. With supplied intrinsics, the system processes KITTI-02 at 12.70 FPS with 3.21 GB peak GPU memory.

</details>


### [32] [Guidance Is a Field, Not a Scalar: Interval-Integrated Classifier-Free Guidance for One-Step Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07566)
*Zijie Meng,Xixin Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AGAVE 将随噪声、空间内容和帧位置变化的无分类器引导编译进 MeanFlow 训练目标，实现无额外推理成本的单步视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单步 MeanFlow 模型通常使用固定或单标量 CFG，无法利用多步采样中的动态引导调度；而最佳引导强度可能随噪声水平、空间内容和视频帧位置变化。

Method: 提出区间积分 CFG 恒等式，将任意引导场吸收到噪声区间上的平均速度目标中；AGAVE 结合可学习的时间调度、运动显著性空间调制和帧位置非对称性，新增参数少于十个标量。

Result: 摘要称，该方法不存在引导调度离散化误差；在 VBench、UCF-101 和 MSR-VTT 上优于所有单步基线，并达到多步引导采样的表现。未提供具体指标或实验设置。

Conclusion: 区间积分使动态 CFG 能够在一次函数求值中实现，为兼顾单步生成效率与引导灵活性提供了方案；具体性能增益及理论主张的适用条件仍需查阅全文。

Abstract: One-step generators built on MeanFlow synthesize a video in a single function evaluation, yet inherit a rigid treatment of classifier-free guidance (CFG): the guidance scale is frozen during training or, at best, exposed as a single scalar. This conflicts with mounting evidence that the optimal guidance is not a constant but a field that varies with the noise level, with spatial content, and, for video, with frame position. Every existing remedy presupposes a multi-step sampler along which guidance can be re-scheduled--an assumption one-step models violate by construction. We resolve this tension through a single observation: because the MeanFlow target is an integral over a noise interval, an arbitrary guidance field can be absorbed into the target itself and compiled into one evaluation. We formalize this as the interval-integrated CFG identity, prove that the compiled guidance carries no schedule-discretization error and can thus match or exceed any finite multi-step sampler, and instantiate it as AGAVE, which equips the average-velocity field with a learnable temporal schedule, a free motion-saliency spatial modulation, and a frame-position asymmetry. Adding fewer than ten scalars at zero inference cost, AGAVE surpasses all one-step baselines on VBench, UCF-101 and MSR-VTT while matching multi-step guided sampling. Project repository: https://github.com/YMlinfeng/AGAVE.

</details>


### [33] [Integrating Learned Priors through Prior-Space Reparameterization for Physically Based Inverse Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07573)
*Hun Hwangbo,Minjun Park,Yongsung Kim,Wooseok Song,Jungmin Ko,Junghyun Hwang,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过先验引导的材质重参数化，在不改变光度优化目标的情况下改善逆渲染和重光照，并加速优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于物理的逆渲染中，材质与光照分解存在歧义；现有方法通常通过辅助正则化损失利用学习到的材质先验，需要权衡先验约束与光度拟合。

Method: 将多视角材质预测融合到网格，在先验特征空间中构建置换格；预测相似的表面点共享材质变量，并使用纯光度目标联合优化材质、几何与光照。

Result: 在两个学习式预测器、三个合成基准及 Stanford-ORB 上改善材质分解和重光照；在 Synthetic4Relight 和 TensoIR-Synthetic 上，重光照 PSNR 较最强受测网格基线提高 1.5–2.5 dB，并优于使用相同先验的正则化方案。缩短后的联合优化耗时不到一分钟，在 TensoIR-Synthetic 上仍优于所有受测基线。

Conclusion: 材质先验可通过限制优化空间而非修改损失函数发挥作用；共享变量还能汇聚含噪光度梯度，提高跨视角更新的一致性，从而支持更高效的优化。

Abstract: Physically based inverse rendering decomposes appearance into materials and illumination, an ambiguous problem for which learned material predictions provide useful prior information. Most existing methods incorporate these cues through auxiliary regularization losses, which alter the optimization objective and require balancing their influence against photometric fitting. We propose a prior-guided material reparameterization that uses the predicted feature relationships to restrict the optimization space while leaving the objective unchanged. We fuse multi-view material predictions onto a mesh and construct a permutohedral lattice in the resulting prior feature space. Surface points with similar predictions share material variables, which are optimized jointly with geometry and illumination using a purely photometric objective. Experiments with two learned predictors on three synthetic benchmarks and Stanford-ORB demonstrate improved material decomposition and relighting. Our method improves relighting PSNR by 1.5-2.5 dB over the strongest evaluated mesh-based baseline on Synthetic4Relight and TensoIR-Synthetic, and outperforms a regularization loss using the same prior in controlled comparisons. We further show that our reparameterization pools noisy photometric gradients, producing updates that are more consistent across sampled views. This consistency enables a shortened joint optimization schedule that takes less than a minute while retaining relighting quality above all evaluated baselines on TensoIR-Synthetic.

</details>


### [34] [Georeferenced Cross-View 3D Reconstruction from Ground and Satellite Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07575)
*David G. Shatwell,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: X-VGGT 联合处理地面图像和地理配准卫星图像，实现稠密三维重建与地理定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角定位方法通常只定位单个地面相机，无法重建稠密几何；前馈多视角重建模型则通常只能输出局部坐标系下的结果。

Method: 采用前馈跨视角几何 Transformer，联合输入多张地面图像和一张地理配准卫星图像，预测相机位姿、稠密三维点图及由重力方向、尺度、偏航角和平移参数化的场景级变换，将局部重建投影到卫星图像平面。

Result: 作者提出联合任务评估协议；摘要称，在多个跨视角数据集上，该方法生成准确的地理配准重建，并优于既有跨视角定位和多视角三维重建模型，但未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 联合建模跨视角定位与三维重建，可将局部几何与地理参考信息相结合，实现全场景地理配准。

Abstract: Georeferenced 3D reconstruction from ground images requires recovering not only the local scene geometry, but also localizing every camera center and 3D point in a geographic coordinate frame. Existing cross-view geo-localization methods are able to estimate the GPS coordinate of a single ground camera, but do not reconstruct dense 3D geometry, while recent feed-forward multi-view 3D reconstruction models predict point maps and camera poses only in a local coordinate frame. We introduce X-VGGT, a feed-forward cross-view geometry transformer that unifies these tasks by jointly processing a set of ground-level images and a georeferenced satellite image to predict ground-view camera poses, dense 3D point maps, and a scene-level transform parameterized by gravity, scale, yaw and translation. This transform projects the local reconstruction into the satellite plane, resulting in the geo-registration of all ground images with the georeferenced satellite image. We further propose an evaluation protocol for this joint task and show that, across multiple cross-view datasets, X-VGGT produces accurate georeferenced reconstructions and outperforms prior cross-view geo-localization and multi-view 3D reconstruction models.

</details>


### [35] [Subspace Learning with Outlier Exposure for Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07580)
*Hongxu Shen,Kun Fang,Jiahui Zheng,Jian Sun,Qinghua Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RecOE通过学习分布内数据与辅助异常数据的判别性特征子空间，利用重建误差之差进行分布外检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常暴露方法主要关注预测输出层，未充分利用特征空间中区分分布内与分布外样本的信息。

Method: 分别学习分布内数据和辅助异常数据的特征子空间，最小化各自在对应子空间中的重建误差；通过样本级目标抑制在对立子空间中的重建，并以正交正则化减少子空间重叠。推理时以两个子空间的重建误差之差作为检测分数。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验表明，RecOE优于近期强基线；额外开销较小，主要来自两个小型子空间基矩阵。摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 利用具有判别性的特征子空间及重建误差，可以有效提升基于异常暴露的分布外检测性能，同时保持较低额外开销。

Abstract: Out-of-Distribution (OoD) detection is pivotal for reliable deployments of Deep Neural Networks (DNNs), aiming to distinguish anomalous samples from the In-Distribution (InD) data in training. By introducing outliers as surrogate OoD data to finetune DNNs, Outlier Exposure (OE) has greatly advanced OoD detection in recent years. Existing OE-based research remains predominantly logit-centric, either by generating diverse outliers or by regularizing them with objectives operating at the prediction-output level. Nonetheless, these approaches leave much of the rich information in feature spaces untapped. In this work, we propose an OE-based detection framework, named RecOE, that learns appropriate feature subspaces from InD data and auxiliary outliers, such that the InD-OoD discrepancy can be effectively characterized through the learned feature subspaces. Specifically, the feature subspaces are optimized to capture the dominant variations of InD and outliers by minimizing reconstruction errors therein. Besides, a sample-level objective discourages reconstruction in the opposing subspace, while an orthogonality regularizer reduces subspace overlap, further promoting discriminative subspaces. At inference, the difference in reconstruction errors between the learned subspaces serves as the OoD detection score. Extensive experiments show the superiority of RecOE on multiple benchmarks, in which significant performance gains are achieved over recent strong baselines with only slight extra overhead from two small-size matrices of subspace bases. The source code is available at https://github.com/shenhongxu123/RecOE.

</details>


### [36] [TACT-KV: Tri-Axis Cosine Transform for Compressing Volumetric KV Caches in Medical VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07593)
*Chongyu Qu,Ritchie Zhao,Yufan He,Dong Yang,Zhengyi Lu,Junchao Zhu,Tianyuan Yao,Juming Xiong,Junlin Guo,Yanfan Zhu,Yuechen Yang,Daguang Xu,Bennett Allan Landman,Yucheng Tang,Yuankai Huo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TACT-KV利用三维空间相关性压缩视觉语言模型的KV缓存，在1比特设置下减少预测翻转，并在相同显存条件下将单GPU解码批量最多提升8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维CT或MRI产生大量视觉令牌，KV缓存随批量和上下文长度线性增长；现有压缩方法未充分利用缓存中的三轴空间结构。

Method: 无需校准：对KV缓存应用可分离三维离散余弦变换，通过反向注水算法按频率分量能量分配各层比特预算，再使用固定标量码本量化；并推导自适应比特分配的失真降低与频谱集中程度之间的关系。

Result: 在六个医学视觉语言模型和两个三维CT基准的12组组合中，11组取得主要对比中最低的1比特预测翻转率；四个通用视觉语言模型的视频任务也呈现类似优势。相同高带宽显存下，单GPU解码批量最多扩大8倍。

Conclusion: 利用三轴结构和频域能量集中性可以有效压缩KV缓存，其收益从三维医学影像推广到视频任务；摘要未提供完整数值、统计显著性或计算开销。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) applied to 3D medical scans face a structural memory wall. A single CT or MRI volume produces tens of thousands of vision tokens, and the resulting key-value (KV) cache scales linearly with both batch and context. Existing KV compression methods operate on the cache's feature or token statistics, or apply 1D and 2D transforms, leaving the three-axis spatial structure in the cache unexploited. We propose TACT-KV (Tri-Axis Cosine Transform), a calibration-free KV compression method that exploits strong spatial correlations in visual KV states along all three anatomical axes of 3D scans. This correlation concentrates the KV cache energy under a frequency-domain transform. TACT-KV applies a separable 3D discrete cosine transform to the KV cache, allocates a per-layer bit budget across frequency bins via reverse water-filling (more bits to high-energy components), and quantizes each bin with a fixed scalar codebook. We derive a close-form relation: the distortion reduction from adaptive bit allocation is dominated by the spectral concentration of the transformed cache. Across six medical VLMs and two 3D CT benchmarks, TACT-KV achieves the lowest 1-bit prediction-flip rate on eleven of twelve model and dataset pairs in our main comparison. The same advantage reproduces on four general-purpose VLMs evaluated on video tasks, indicating that the benefit of exploiting three-axis structure generalizes beyond medical imaging. At equal high-bandwidth memory (HBM), TACT-KV scales the decoding batch size on a single GPU by up to $8\times$.

</details>


### [37] [FisherDistill: Diffusion Distribution Distillation via Closed-Form Fisher Critics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07604)
*Mahesh Bhosale,Yuanhao Zhai,Siwei Lyu,Junsong Yuan,David Doermann,Defang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FisherDistill利用冻结教师模型的特征和Fisher线性判别方向，在无需训练额外判别器的情况下改进扩散蒸馏生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高度蒸馏的生成器虽能以一次或少量网络函数评估生成图像，但其分布仍与多步教师存在差距；现有对抗式优化通常需要额外训练判别器。

Method: 提取冻结教师的中间特征，估计真实与生成样本特征的一阶和二阶统计量，以闭式解计算相对于类内变异最能区分两类特征的Fisher方向，并将其作为原蒸馏目标的补充优化信号。

Result: 在多个蒸馏框架中，该方法持续优于对应的蒸馏基线，并显著缩小了与对抗式优化方法的差距；摘要未提供具体指标或数值。

Conclusion: 基于教师特征的Fisher判别信号可作为轻量级替代方案，改善蒸馏模型，而无需额外的对抗判别器及其优化循环。

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion distillation have enabled high-quality image generation in only one or a few network function evaluations (NFEs). Despite their efficiency, aggressively distilled generators often retain a residual distribution gap from their multi-step teachers, motivating recent methods to introduce adversarial refinement with a learned discriminator. In this work, we ask whether such refinement necessarily requires a learned adversarial discriminator. We introduce \textbf{FisherDistill}, a simple alternative based on Fisher linear discriminant analysis. Using intermediate representations from the frozen teacher, we estimate the first- and second-order statistics of real and generated samples and derive a closed-form Fisher direction that maximally separates their feature distributions relative to within-class variation. This direction provides a lightweight refinement signal that complements the original distillation objective without requiring an additional adversarial discriminator or its optimization loop. Across multiple distillation frameworks, our approach consistently improves generation quality over their respective distilled baselines and substantially closes the gap to adversarially refined counterparts. Code: https://bhosalems.github.io/FisherDistill/

</details>


### [38] [Towards Real-Time Full-Waveform LiDAR Transformers via Intensity-Guided Token Reduction and Physics-Aware Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07613)
*Kotaro Oishi,Kazuma Ikeda,Ryosei Hara,Ryo Yoshida,Mariko Isogawa,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 利用全波形激光雷达的物理结构进行令牌压缩和数据增强，实现实时推理并提升准确率与场景泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全波形激光雷达 Transformer 受低强度背景区域的冗余令牌拖累，计算成本高；同时，标注数据稀缺限制了其对未见场景的泛化。

Method: 提出 FWL-ToPM，根据强度分布对令牌进行剪枝与合并，同时保留关键波形信息；提出 FWLAug，在保留各射线飞行时间波形及视锥几何结构的前提下进行数据增强，以发挥正则化作用。
