Result: 在 Ghost-FWL 基准上，FWL-ToPM 相比此前最先进方法实现超过 8 倍加速，达到实时吞吐，同时 F1 分数提高近 9 个百分点。FWLAug 进一步提升标准基准及未见场景分布外测试集上的性能。

Conclusion: 结合物理结构感知的令牌压缩与数据增强，可同时改善全波形激光雷达感知的效率、准确率和泛化能力，推动实际部署。

Abstract: LiDAR is a critical sensor for autonomous driving and robotics, yet it remains vulnerable to adverse conditions such as fog, rain, transparent objects, and low-reflectance surfaces. Full-waveform LiDAR (FWL) addresses these limitations by capturing the complete return waveform as a histogram, preserving rich reflection characteristics including multi-path echoes and weak signals. While recent Transformer-based approaches have demonstrated promise on FWL data, two fundamental bottlenecks remain: high computational cost from redundant tokens in low-intensity background regions, and limited generalization due to scarce annotated training data. We address both bottlenecks by exploiting the physical structure inherent to FWL data. First, we propose FWL-ToPM, a token pruning and merging mechanism that leverages intensity distributions to selectively reduce tokens while retaining critical waveform content. Second, we introduce FWLAug, a data augmentation framework designed as a regularization method that preserves waveform structure, retaining the Time-of-Flight waveform of each ray and the frustum geometry, to improve generalization to unseen scenes. Evaluated on the Ghost-FWL benchmark, FWL-ToPM achieves over 8 times speedup at real-time throughput alongside a nearly 9-point F1 gain over the prior state of the art, establishing the first real-time FWL Transformer that simultaneously improves accuracy. FWLAug further improves performance on both the standard benchmark and an out-of-distribution test set of unseen scenes. Together, these methods push FWL-based perception toward practical deployment. The code and the OOD dataset are publicly available and can be accessed via the project page: https://keio-csg.github.io/FWL-ToPM

</details>


### [39] [TRACEMARK: Editor-Aware Watermarking for User Attribution in Text-Guided Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07632)
*Xiaodong Wu,Xiangman Li,Jianbing Ni,Rongxing Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tracemark 通过针对图像编辑过程联合训练水印编码器和检测器，在不修改编辑器的情况下，从编辑后图像识别请求用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像编辑服务的用户请求记录不会随图像传播，而语义编辑可能破坏常规水印，导致下载和分享后的图像难以追溯请求者。

Method: 共享编码器在编辑前嵌入用户专属信号；共享检测器将编辑后图像特征与学习得到的参考向量匹配以识别用户。两者通过参数固定的编辑器联合训练，结合用户识别损失与保真损失；每个图像域和编辑指令分别训练模型，并由注册用户共享。

Result: 在三个图像域上，编辑器感知训练改善了参考向量匹配和二进制水印解码的用户恢复效果；针对不同图像域和指令分别训练的模型在500名用户中达到87.5%的平均识别准确率。无需重新训练，水印模块在使用不同编辑器时仍可恢复用户信息。

Conclusion: 将编辑过程纳入水印训练有助于生成能经受编辑的用户标识，实现无需修改编辑器的用户归因；摘要未说明跨编辑器测试的具体准确率。

Abstract: An image editing service records which user requests each edit, but the edited image itself does not contain this account information. Once the image is downloaded and shared, the service cannot identify the requesting user from the image alone. Embedding a user-specific watermark could enable such attribution, but semantic editing may erase the signal. We propose Tracemark, an editor-aware watermarking framework for identifying users from edited images. A shared encoder embeds a user-specific signal before editing, and a shared detector recovers the user by comparing features of the edited image with learned reference vectors. Both components are trained jointly through an editor with fixed parameters. User identification errors encourage the encoder to produce watermarks that survive editing, while fidelity losses limit changes to the input image and intended edit. Each image domain and editing instruction uses a separately trained model shared across registered users. Experiments on three image domains show that editor-aware training improves user recovery with both reference-vector matching and binary watermark decoding. Separately trained models reach 87.5% mean identification accuracy among 500 users. When tested with a different editor without retraining, the watermark modules still recover user information. These results demonstrate that learning watermarks through the editing process enables user attribution without modifying the editor.

</details>


### [40] [Neural Image Compression with Internal-External Prior Fusion and Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07644)
*Tiancheng Si,Wenlong Xu,Dahua Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出融合图像内部先验与共享外部字典的学习式图像压缩模型，以较低复杂度实现领先的率失真性能和快速编解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文模型主要依赖当前图像的内部先验，忽视自然图像之间共享的常见结构和典型纹理。

Method: 基于四叉树空间—通道联合建模，使用编码器与解码器共享的可学习字典作为外部先验；通过基于RWKV的RSCPF模块以线性复杂度融合内外先验，并使用仅在训练阶段启用的SDPA机制对齐先验。单网络支持64个质量等级，采用定制CUDA算子加速。

Result: 在七个基准上，相较VTM-22.0-Intra平均节省21.65%的BD-Rate；模型参数量为29.9M，编码和解码时间分别为61毫秒和52毫秒。摘要称其在先进神经编解码器中速度最快。

Conclusion: 融合共享外部先验与图像内部先验可改善学习式图像压缩的率失真表现，同时兼顾可变码率和编解码效率；具体实验条件及各基准结果需查阅全文。

Abstract: Existing context models for learned image com- pression rely primarily on internal priors from the current image, overlooking common patterns and typical textures shared across natural images. To address this limitation, we propose an Internal-External Prior Fusion-Alignment (IE-PFA) context model built upon quadtree spatial-channel joint modeling, where a learnable dictionary shared by the encoder and decoder serves as an external prior. Specifically, an RWKV-based Spatial- Channel Prior Fusion (RSCPF) module efficiently fuses inter- nal and external priors along spatial and channel dimensions with linear complexity, while a training-only Self-Distilled Prior Alignment (SDPA) mechanism aligns them without inference overhead. Moreover, our model supports variable-rate coding over 64 quality levels within a single network and is accelerated by custom CUDA operators. Experiments on seven benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art rate-distortion performance, achiev- ing an average BD-Rate saving of 21.65% over VTM-22.0-Intra, with only 29.9M parameters and the fastest coding speed among advanced neural codecs (61 ms encoding and 52 ms decoding). Code is available at https://github.com/stcnjtech/DCVC-X-Intra.

</details>


### [41] [UniGADD: Universal Generator-Agnostic Deepfake Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07647)
*Bernard Tomczyk,Agil Aghasanli,Plamen Angelov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniGADD通过两阶段监督学习，兼顾已知生成器的检测准确率与未知生成器的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测方法需要同时解决已知生成器上的准确检测和面对未知生成技术时的泛化问题。

Method: 第一阶段使用对比损失，从真实图像及已知生成器的伪造图像中学习判别性特征嵌入；第二阶段仅使用真实样本，通过增大簇间距离、提高簇内紧凑性来优化嵌入空间。

Result: 据摘要报告，该方法在已知生成器上的准确率与现有最先进方法相当，在未知生成器上的表现显著优于这些方法；摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 针对真实样本优化特征空间有助于提升深度伪造检测对未知生成技术的鲁棒性，体现出潜在的可扩展性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce Universal Generator-Agnostic Deepfake Detector (UniGADD), a novel method that employs supervised learning to achieve high accuracy on known deepfake generators, while maintaining robust performance on previously unseen ones. The proposed approach follows a two-stage optimisation process. In the first stage, a contrastive loss encourages the model to learn discriminative feature embeddings from real and known fake images, resulting in strong performance within the training domain. In the second stage, the embedding space is refined by promoting inter-cluster separation and intra-cluster compactness, applied exclusively to real samples. This refinement enhances generalisability, enabling the method to exhibit improved robustness against unseen deepfake generation techniques. UniGADD achieves accuracy on par with state-of-the-art methods for known generators, while significantly outperforming them on unseen cases, demonstrating its scalability and practical applicability for adversarial content detection.

</details>


### [42] [Generator-Aligned Reward Modeling with Target-Aware Bounded Optimization for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07648)
*Ziqiao Weng,Yuanzhi Liang,Haibing Huang,Weidong Cai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文通过生成器对齐的奖励建模和目标感知的有界优化，增强视觉生成模型的目标属性，同时控制画质退化与属性泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励引导后训练面临真实数据与生成器输出分布不匹配、属性监督受无关内容混杂，以及无界奖励最大化导致过度优化和生成质量下降的问题。

Method: 提出GARM与TABO框架：GARM通过生成器支持集匹配和内容匹配的真实对照构建奖励监督；TABO依据真实目标正样本校准平滑的奖励上界，兼容直接奖励梯度和策略优化。此外，提出面向流模型视频优化的TA-GRPO，结合固定分阶段优势缩放、从生成器到目标的课程学习及时间步均衡的信用分配。

Result: 摘要报告GARM增强了目标属性表达并保持有竞争力的生成质量；相比无约束奖励最大化，TABO更好地平衡属性增强与视觉质量；相比原始Flow-GRPO，TA-GRPO提升目标增强效果，并减少中性提示下的非预期属性泄漏。摘要未提供具体量化结果。

Conclusion: 与生成器分布对齐且聚焦目标属性的奖励监督，结合由真实数据校准的受控优化，有助于实现更可靠的定向视觉生成。

Abstract: Post-training aims to enhance desired attributes in visual generative models while preserving their general generation capabilities. Reward-guided post-training faces two challenges: real training data may mismatch the generator's output distribution, and attribute supervision may be confounded by unrelated content. Unbounded reward maximization can also cause overoptimization and degrade generation quality. We propose a framework combining Generator-Aligned Reward Modeling (GARM) and Target-Aware Bounded Optimization (TABO). GARM mitigates distribution mismatch through generator-support matching and reduces content confounding using content-matched real contrasts, yielding generator-relevant, attribute-focused reward supervision. TABO maps the learned reward to a smooth, upper-bounded function calibrated from real target-positive examples. This transformation is independent of the optimization algorithm and supports both direct reward-gradient and policy-based optimization. We further introduce Target-Aware GRPO (TA-GRPO), an adaptation of Flow-GRPO that combines fixed stage-wise advantage scaling, a generator-to-target curriculum, and timestep-balanced credit assignment for flow-based video optimization. Experiments show that GARM improves target-attribute expression while maintaining competitive generation quality. TABO better balances attribute enhancement and visual quality than unconstrained reward maximization, while TA-GRPO improves target enhancement over vanilla Flow-GRPO and reduces unintended attribute leakage on neutral prompts. These results highlight the importance of generator-aligned reward supervision and controlled, data-calibrated optimization for targeted visual generation.

</details>


### [43] [ArtCrafter: Feed-Forward Generation of Articulated 3D Object with Analytic Joint Derivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07658)
*Minh Canh Tu,Quang-Binh Nguyen,Khoi Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArtCrafter利用静止态与打开态两张图像，联合生成部件网格并解析推导关节参数，实现可控的关节式3D对象重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人仿真与训练需要关节式3D资产，但从普通图像恢复相互一致的部件几何和运动学结构仍具挑战。

Method: 基于DiT的前馈生成模型采用1+2N槽位：一个共享基座槽和N对静止／打开状态槽；通过三元注意力约束跨状态几何一致性，并以点云排斥损失减少部件穿透；再根据对应部件间的刚体变换解析推导URDF关节类型、轴、原点和范围。

Result: 摘要报告，在同分布及分布外数据集上，相同双状态输入条件下，ArtCrafter的几何质量和关节精度优于现有方法；与采用不同输入或范式的方法相比也具有竞争力。摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 联合建模双状态部件几何，并通过解析方式恢复运动学参数，是生成几何与运动学一致的关节式3D资产的一种有效方案。

Abstract: Articulated 3D assets are essential for robotic simulation and training, yet their generation from casual images poses a key challenge: recovering part geometry and kinematics that remain mutually consistent. We present ArtCrafter, a novel feed-forward generative model for controllable generation of articulated 3D objects from a pair of images, one depicting the object in the rest state and the other in the open state. At the core of ArtCrafter is a DiT-based generative model that jointly produces part-level meshes for both articulation states in a single forward pass. Inspired by Slot Attention, its denoising process operates on a $1+2N$ slot layout comprising one shared base slot and $N$ paired rest/open slots, encouraging each pair to bind to the same kinematic part. To ensure geometrically consistent and disjoint part generation, we introduce Triplet Attention to enforce cross-state geometric consistency between paired rest/open slots anchored through the shared base slot, and a repulsion loss $\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{rep}}$ on decoded part point clouds to prevent inter-part penetration throughout denoising. Given the resulting paired meshes, URDF joint parameters---type, axis, origin, and range---are derived analytically from the rigid transformation between each rest/open pair, providing an interpretable alternative to learned joint-parameter regression. Extensive experiments on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution datasets demonstrate that ArtCrafter outperforms existing method under the same dual-state setting in geometry quality and articulation precision, while remaining competitive with methods operating under different input settings and paradigms.

</details>


### [44] [Which Objects Stay? Learning Hierarchical Persistence Beliefs for Lifelong Open-Vocabulary Object Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07662)
*Thanh Nguyen Canh,Haolan Zhang,Nak Young Chong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiPer通过相似实例检索和对象级在线学习，在无需类别标签的情况下估计物体位置持久性，并在模拟与真实环境中优于比较方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终身物体地图需要判断离开视野的物体是否仍在原位置；现有滤波器依赖基于类别的生存先验，难以可靠适用于开放词汇场景。

Method: 检索视觉和几何特征相似的训练实例，利用其观测历史估计初始生存率；以此初始化生存概率的共轭Beta信念，并在Rosen持久性滤波框架下结合检测与漏检进行闭式更新。物体在新位置被重新识别时，随物体迁移其信念，以学习个体移动性。

Result: 在120条留出模拟序列中，HiPer持续取得比较方法中的最佳表现；检索先验优于CLIP标签先验，对象级在线学习进一步提升性能。在47个留出的3RScan真实环境中，检索先验也优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: 基于数据检索的无类别先验与对象级持续学习，有助于提升开放词汇终身物体地图中的持久性估计；摘要未提供具体指标或提升幅度。

Abstract: Lifelong object maps must estimate whether an object remains at its previously observed location after it leaves view. Existing persistence filters rely on class-based survival priors, making them difficult to apply reliably in open-vocabulary settings. We introduce HiPer, a hierarchical persistence filter that derives persistence beliefs directly from data rather than semantic categories. For a new object, HiPer retrieves visually and geometrically similar training instances and estimates an initial survival rate from their observed histories, without requiring class labels. This estimate initializes a conjugate Beta belief over the object's survival probability, which is updated in closed form using detections and missed observations under Rosen's persistence-filter framework. When an object is re-identified at a new location, its persistence belief is transferred with the object, enabling the map to learn object-specific mobility over time. Experiments on 120 held-out simulated sequences show that HiPer consistently achieves the best performance among competing methods. Retrieval-based priors outperform CLIP-derived label priors, and object-specific online learning provides further gains. Results on 47 held-out 3RScan environments demonstrate that the proposed retrieval-based prior also outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in real-world settings.

</details>


### [45] [CoGenRec: A Unified Generative Framework for Complementary Vision Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07665)
*Yapeng Meng,Yuguo Chen,Xiangru Chen,Shaojun Xu,Xuezhen Wang,Lijian Wang,Zheyu Yang,Yihan Lin,Luping Shi,Rong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoGenRec 利用互补视觉传感器的双路径测量与视频扩散先验，统一实现去模糊、插帧和 HDR 融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端运动与高动态范围场景受时间分辨率、空间细节、信噪比和动态范围之间的权衡限制，传统相机容易出现时间欠采样、运动模糊和传感器饱和。

Method: 结合空间稠密、时间稀疏的长曝光 RGB 测量与时间稠密、空间稀疏的短曝光时空差分测量；通过曝光感知外观建模和双向时间传播提取多尺度时空特征，以约束视频扩散模型，并将特征注入 VAE 解码器以恢复细节。

Result: 在 SportsSloMo-CVS 上，相比最强通用基线，去模糊、插帧和 HDR 融合的 PSNR 分别提高 4.40、3.89 和 5.04 dB；在真实 CVS 采集的 129 个未见场景中也表现出较好的感知质量。

Conclusion: 互补物理测量与生成式视频先验相结合，可为复杂运动和光照条件下的多任务高质量重建提供统一方法；摘要未提供运行效率或更多真实场景定量结果。

Abstract: Capturing scenes with extreme motion and high dynamic range is fundamentally constrained by trade-offs among temporal resolution, spatial detail, signal-to-noise ratio, and dynamic range under practical bandwidth, power, and quantum-efficiency limits. Conventional frame-based cameras therefore suffer from temporal undersampling, exposure-induced motion blur, and sensor saturation. The Complementary Vision Sensor (CVS) addresses these limitations by decomposing visual information across two complementary pathways, a spatially dense, temporally sparse, long-exposure RGB pathway and a temporally dense, spatially sparse, short-exposure patiotemporal difference pathway. Their complementary measurements provide appearance, structural, and motion cues across a wide dynamic range that enable reconstruction beyond conventional imaging. We present CoGenRec, a complementarity-driven generative reconstruction framework that unifies video deblurring, high-temporal-resolution frame interpolation, and HDR fusion. CoGenRec encodes complementary measurements into multi-scale spatiotemporal features using exposure-aware appearance modeling and bidirectional temporal propagation. These features constrain a video diffusion model and are injected into the VAE decoder to recover fine structures lost during latent-space compression, combining the scene fidelity of physical measurements with the appearance recovery capabilities of learned video priors. On SportsSloMo-CVS, CoGenRec improves PSNR over the strongest general-purpose baseline by 4.40 dB for deblurring, 3.89 dB for frame interpolation, and 5.04 dB for HDR fusion, respectively. It also achieves strong perceptual quality on 129 unseen scenes captured with real CVS. These results establish a unified approach to advance high-quality reconstruction under challenging motion and illumination conditions. Project page: https://cogenrec.github.io/.

</details>


### [46] [Egocentric Action Recognition with Objects and Vision-Language Affordances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07673)
*Raúl Boix-Aldeguer,Manuel Benavent-Lledo,David Ortiz-Perez,Jose Garcia-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过融合冻结的 SlowFast 视频专家与基于注意力的物体专家，并利用视觉语言模型零样本生成可供性，在 MECCANO 数据集上将动作识别 Top-1 准确率从 44.9% 提升至 49.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称动作往往由交互物体定义，但现有方法较少显式建模物体；此外，直接使用仅包含动作相关物体的标注框进行评估会泄露活跃物体信息，不能充分反映真实检测条件。

Method: 采用后期融合结合冻结的 SlowFast 视频专家和注意力物体专家；利用视觉语言模型零样本生成物体可供性及判断手是否操纵物体的交互可供性。训练使用标注框，评估使用官方活跃物体检测器的检测框，并以标注框结果作为上界。

Result: MECCANO 上，视频专家 Top-1 为 44.9%；加入物体信息后为 49.0%，再加入两类可供性后为 49.9%，优于 SlowFast 和物体感知 RGB 基线。未使用可供性时，标注框与检测框结果分别为 68.4% 和 49.0%，约一半的性能下降来自非活跃物体检测。

Conclusion: 显式物体建模和零样本可供性有助于第一人称动作识别；检测噪声，特别是非活跃物体，是制约实际性能的重要因素，标注框评估可能高估效果。

Abstract: Action recognition, and more specifically egocentric action recognition, is a core task in computer vision, with applications such as monitoring the daily life activities of elderly people and task assistance. Recent methods rely on visual modalities such as RGB video or depth. However, in egocentric video, an action is largely defined by its objects, which these methods rarely model. We propose a method that combines a frozen SlowFast video expert with an attention-based object expert by late fusion. Each object is represented with its affordances, that is, what can be done with it, generated zero-shot by a vision-language model. Since datasets such as MECCANO annotate only the objects involved in each action, evaluating with these boxes tells the model which objects are active. We therefore train on annotated boxes and evaluate the method with detections from the official active-object detector, and with annotated boxes as an upper bound. Without affordances, our fusion reaches 68.4% Top-1 with annotated boxes but 49.0% with detections. About half of this drop comes from non-active detections. To remove them, the same vision-language model generates an interaction affordance that decides whether a hand is manipulating each detected object. On MECCANO, objects raise the Top-1 of the video expert from 44.9% to 49.0%, and the two affordances raise it to 49.9%, above SlowFast and object-aware RGB baselines. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [47] [GEKey: Learning Geodesic Eccentric 3D Keypoints via Self-Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07677)
*Pierre Onghena,Santiago Velasco-Forero,Beatriz Marcotegui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GEKey利用测地偏心率变换生成伪标签，自监督学习点云关键点，在检测、对齐及鲁棒性方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督式关键点检测依赖人工标注；需要利用物体内在几何结构，实现无需人工标注且保留类内差异的关键点检测。

Method: 采用对SE(3)变换不变的测地偏心率变换，识别实例特有的显著点并将其作为伪标签，训练预测结果与物体内在结构一致的模型，同时平衡实例级与类别级结构。

Result: 在ShapeNetCoreV2、KeypointNet、CAPE、ITOP和DeformingThings4D上的实验表明，该方法在关键点检测和对齐方面达到先进水平，并能应对噪声、下采样、形变及真实场景条件；摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 基于内在几何的自监督伪标签可有效支持点云关键点检测与对齐，同时兼顾效率、鲁棒性和类内变化。

Abstract: Point clouds represent 3D object geometry as sets of spatial coordinates. A fundamental task in their analysis is keypoint detection, i.e., identifying a sparse subset of points that captures the most salient features. While supervised methods rely on manual annotations, unsupervised approaches leverage intrinsic geometric properties to localize keypoints without human input. We introduce GEKey, a self-supervised framework that uses the geodesic eccentricity transform, an SE(3)-invariant geometric measure, to identify instance-specific salient points. These points serve as pseudo-labels to train a model that aligns its predictions with the object's intrinsic structure. GEKey is simple, efficient, and robust, preserving intra-class variation by balancing instance-specific and category-level structure. Extensive experiments on ShapeNetCoreV2, KeypointNet, CAPE, ITOP, and DeformingThings4D show state-of-the-art performance in both keypoint detection and alignment, with strong resilience to noise, downsampling, deformations, and real-world conditions. The code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/Pierreoo/GEKey.

</details>


### [48] [DEQ-Det: Dual Evidence Queries for Few-Shot Analog IC Device Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07682)
*Zidong Zhao,Rong Huang,Chengxing Fan,Jie Hu,Malik Haris,Ghazal A. Fahmy,Morteza Mousazadeh,Guoqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DEQ-Det 利用同一支持图像中的前景与背景证据，在少样本模拟集成电路版图器件检测中提升精度并抑制误检。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 器件几何形态多变，且与虚设结构或布线结构外观相似；仅依赖前景相似性难以区分目标与干扰。

Method: 无需额外标注，从支持图像构建互补的前景和背景参考，分别表征目标形态与周边版图模式，并共同指导查询选择和区域验证。

Result: 在五个未见器件类别、三个随机种子的评估中，10-shot 和 30-shot 的宏平均 AP50 分别为 39.41 和 53.03，较所列最强基线分别提高 3.97 和 9.71 个百分点。匹配的 30-shot 消融实验表明，RoI 背景证据在召回率相近时减少了汇总误检。

Conclusion: 结合目标外观与版图上下文有助于提升少样本器件检测性能；消融结果支持背景证据对抑制干扰的作用。

Abstract: Detecting analog integrated circuit (IC) devices in layout images supports circuit analysis and inspection. With only a few annotated examples, variation in device geometry and visual similarity to dummy or routing structures make reliable detection difficult. Foreground similarity alone can highlight plausible targets without distinguishing them from competing layout patterns. We propose Dual Evidence Queries for Detection (DEQ-Det), which constructs complementary foreground and background references from the same support images without additional annotations. Foreground references capture target morphology, while selected background references characterize surrounding layout patterns. Both sources guide query selection and region verification, combining target appearance with layout context. Across five held-out device categories and three random seeds, DEQ-Det achieves macro AP50 of 39.41 and 53.03 at 10 and 30 shots, exceeding the strongest listed baselines by 3.97 and 9.71 points, respectively. A matched 30-shot ablation further shows that RoI background evidence reduces pooled false positives at comparable recall, supporting its role in interference suppression.

</details>


### [49] [ReferClaw: Disambiguating User Intent for Egocentric Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07683)
*Hangyu Qin,Junbin Xiao,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReferClaw 通过多智能体主动澄清用户意图，提升第一人称流视频问答的准确性与视觉定位表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户的简短提问常存在指代歧义，仅凭视频和对话历史无法唯一确定意图；现有 VideoQA 系统直接选择单一解释，可能给出看似合理但不符合用户意图的答案。

Method: 将流视频问答建模为交互过程，由三个协作智能体分别识别候选意图、请求澄清并依据确定的意图和视觉证据作答；采用有边界的成本感知策略，权衡预期信息增益与用户负担，并限制澄清轮次和问题复杂度；通过意图特定技能更新及过程奖励引导的策略优化学习，并进行智能体级信用分配。

Result: 在增强的第一人称问答基准上，相较现有基线，回答准确率及回答与视觉定位的联合表现均有提升，并展示了跨数据集零样本迁移能力；摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 主动交互澄清是解决第一人称流视频助手在线用户意图歧义的一种可行途径。

Abstract: Users of egocentric AI assistants often ask short, ambiguous questions whose intended referents cannot be uniquely resolved from video and dialogue history. For example, "Where did I leave it?" may refer to several previously handled objects. Existing VideoQA systems typically commit to a single interpretation, risking plausible but intention-misaligned answers. We introduce ReferClaw, an agentic framework that formulates egocentric streaming QA as an interactive process: the assistant asks clarifying questions to resolve user intent before answering. Three cooperating agents identify candidate interpretations, request clarification, and generate answers grounded in the resolved intent and visual evidence. A bounded, cost-aware policy balances expected information gain against user effort while limiting clarification turns and question complexity. Referclaw learns from interaction through intent-specific skill updates and process-reward-guided policy optimization with agent-level credit assignment. Evaluations on augmented egocentric QA benchmarks demonstrate improved answer accuracy and joint answer-and-grounding performance over existing baselines, alongside zero-shot transfer across datasets. These findings support interactive clarification as a practical approach to resolving online user intent in egocentric streaming assistance.

</details>


### [50] [InfMasking: A Unified Variational Framework for Synergistic Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07702)
*Liangjian Wen,Qun Dai,Jiang Duan,Yong Dai,Jianzhuang Liu,Dongkai Wang,Jun Wang,Fuyuan Cao,Zenglin Xu,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InfMasking通过无限随机特征掩码和跨视图一致性训练，促进多模态融合模型利用跨模态协同信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 融合模块可能依赖优势模态或模态间共享信息，忽略只有联合观察多个模态才能获得的协同信息。

Method: 融合前随机掩码各模态特征，并约束不同掩码下的融合表示与参考目标一致：无监督训练使用未掩码融合视图，有监督训练使用标签对应的类别代理。将掩码融合表示建模为高斯分布，优化无限掩码期望的闭式变分界；该模型下总体矩差距受掩码诱导协方差控制。

Result: 在所评测基准上，预测性能有所提升；学习到的模态特定表示具有更高的任务标签协同信息平均PID估计，训练后的预测器具有更高的InterSHAP交互归因。摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 多样化部分观测下的一致性约束有助于减少对单一模态或共享信息的依赖，并促进学习有意义的跨模态交互。

Abstract: Multimodal models are expected to exploit synergistic information, that is, information available only when multiple modalities are observed together. In practice, however, a fusion module can fit the training objective by relying on a dominant modality or on information that modalities share, and the synergy between modalities is often left unused. We propose InfMasking, a unified multimodal learning framework based on Infinite Masking. Before fusion, InfMasking randomly masks the features of each modality; each sampled mask produces a different partial combination of cross-modal evidence and a fused representation of the corresponding modality interactions. All masked fused representations are required to remain consistent with a reference target: the unmasked fused view when training is unsupervised, and the class proxy of the task label when labels are available. Maintaining this consistency across diverse partial combinations discourages reliance on a dominant modality or on shared information alone, and drives the fusion module to learn cross-modal interaction patterns that remain meaningful under partial observations. Since the objective is an expectation over infinitely many masks, we model the masked fused representations as Gaussian and optimize closed-form variational bounds, whose population-moment gaps are controlled by the masking-induced covariance under this model. On the evaluated benchmarks, InfMasking improves predictive performance and yields higher mean PID estimates of task-label synergy in learned modality-specific representations, alongside higher InterSHAP interaction attribution in the trained predictors. Code is available at https://github.com/brightest66/InfMasking.

</details>


### [51] [FlyingDrones: A Dataset and Benchmark for Optical Flow Estimation from UAV motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07705)
*Laurenz Ruzicka,Roman Pflugfelder,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无人机光流基准 FlyingDrones，系统评测模型并验证其对真实视频与微调的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远距离小型无人机像素少、对比度低，且背景动态复杂；现有光流基准不能充分反映此类目标。

Method: 通过基于物理的渲染生成稠密光流真值和分割掩码，提出按图像尺寸归一化的 EPE Norm；在三个分辨率下，对按运动和尺寸分层的999个光流图像对评估16类模型，并开展真实视频对齐与微调实验。

Result: 所有模型族均优于零光流基线，模型族间差距随分辨率提高而扩大；同族不同检查点的差异可与族间差异相当或更大。合成与真实表现的排名呈中等显著相关（41个检查点，Spearman ρ=0.63）；使用800对数据微调后，留出集无人机区域误差最多降低37%，四种架构的真实视频定位均得到改善。

Conclusion: FlyingDrones 为远距离无人机光流评估提供了有用基准，训练课程与架构同样重要；合成排名对真实表现有一定预测价值，但不能保证精确排名一致。

Abstract: Optical flow is an important cue for drone analysis in aerial surveillance, airspace monitoring, and long-range target tracking, where motion remains informative when appearance is weak. Estimating the motion of small Unmanned Aerial Vehicles, however, is difficult because distant targets occupy few pixels, have low contrast, and move against complex dynamic backgrounds. Standard optical-flow benchmarks emphasize large, object-centric motions and therefore do not characterize distant aerial targets well. We introduce FlyingDrones, a generated benchmark with dense ground-truth optical flow and segmentation masks derived from physically based rendering, and define resolution-normalized metrics (EPE Norm) that normalize flow by image dimensions. We evaluate 16 state-of-the-art model families on a motion- and size-stratified subset of 999 flow pairs at three resolutions. Every family recovers drone motion better than the zero-flow reference, but the gap between families widens with resolution: at 1920x1080, global-matching, recurrent, transformer, and short-window temporal models separate clearly from pyramid, classical, self-supervised, lightweight, and diffusion models. Within-family checkpoint spread is comparable to or larger than between-family spread, so the training curriculum is as important as the architecture. Two complementary studies test the benchmark beyond synthetic accuracy: a ground-truth-free motion-alignment evaluation on 21 cameras of real UAV footage shows that the synthetic ranking carries a moderate, significant signal about real-world performance (Spearman \r{ho} = 0.63 over 41 checkpoints) while the exact top ranks differ, and a fine-tuning study shows that 800 FlyingDrones pairs reduce held-out drone-region error by up to 37% and improve drone localization on the real footage for all four architectures tested.

</details>


### [52] [AffineGS: Unlocking Accurate Geometry in 3DGS via Spatially Varying Affine Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07709)
*Tze Ho Elden Tse,Jizong Peng,Richard Chen,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出空间变化的仿射校正与法线感知正则化，在无需深度传感器的情况下提升3D高斯溅射的几何精度，同时保持较高的渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯溅射的视觉质量较高，但几何精度不足；现有方法采用单一全局尺度与偏移对齐单目深度先验，无法处理单目深度网络的区域相关分段线性失真，可能导致场景几何形变。

Method: 构建空间变化的仿射校正模型，利用区域平滑且边缘感知的参数场，将单目视差校正为一致的度量深度；进一步通过空间梯度设计法线感知正则项，耦合校正后的视差与3DGS深度及法线。

Result: 摘要报告，在多种实验设置下，该无传感器方法保持了较高的光度质量，显著改善几何精度，并优于现有先进的几何重建方法；摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 针对单目深度先验的区域性失真进行局部校正，结合深度与法线约束，可改善3DGS的几何重建，而不必依赖深度传感器。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become the leading approach for photorealistic novel view synthesis, yet its geometric accuracy lags behind its visual quality. Existing depth-guided methods attempt to resolve this by regularizing the optimization with monocular depth priors, typically using a single global scale-and-shift alignment. Crucially, we discover a fundamental flaw in this assumption: modern monocular depth networks systematically exhibit region-dependent, piecewise-linear distortions. This discovery invalidates standard global alignment strategies, which warp scene geometry by enforcing a single transform. To address this, we introduce a spatially varying affine rectification model that corrects monocular disparity into consistent metric depth using regionally smooth, edge-aware fields. We further derive a normal-aware regularizer that uses spatial gradients to couple the rectified disparity with 3DGS depth and normals. Across diverse experimental settings, our sensor-free method shows strong photometric quality of Gaussian Splatting while substantially improving geometric accuracy and outperforming state-of-the art geometry-focused methods.

</details>


### [53] [Fast and Accurate Hand Estimation in Pixels, Interaction and World Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07711)
*Jinkun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FAH通过直接预测手部关键点和表面点并拟合手部网格，实现高精度、实时的单目手部估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通过预测骨架参数并投影网格获得二维关键点，但直接预测的三维关节与二维预测更一致；利用预测点拟合网格可降低网格误差。

Method: 基于SAM 3D Body的手部分支进行微调和特征蒸馏，预测对应的二维、三维关节及表面点，拟合MANO或MHR-Hand；可选估计解剖部位和接触，推理时无需物体几何；结合因果细化和相机估计实现平滑的世界坐标追踪。

Result: 相较于所评估基线，图像空间对齐、手部几何、接触估计和世界空间追踪均有改善。RTX 4070 Ti SUPER上，默认ViT-L含网格拟合时为65 fps，启用部位和接触估计时为49 fps；最小ViT-S不启用上述功能时为125 fps。

Conclusion: 直接点预测结合参数化网格拟合，可兼顾单目手部估计的精度、功能和实时性；摘要未提供具体误差数值或基线增幅。

Abstract: Monocular hand mesh estimators commonly predict rig parameters and project the mesh to obtain 2D keypoints. We find that directly predicted 3D joints agree better with 2D predictions, and fitting a mesh to predicted points reduces mesh error. We present FAH (Fast and Accurate Hand Estimation), a family of models built on SAM 3D Body's hand branch through fine-tuning and feature distillation. The models predict corresponding 2D and 3D joints and surface points, fit MANO or MHR-Hand, and optionally estimate anatomical parts and contact without object geometry at inference. Causal refinement reduces acceleration error, and camera estimation places the hands in world space. Experiments show improvements in image-space alignment, hand geometry, contact estimation and world-space tracking over the evaluated baselines. The default ViT-L model runs at 65 fps with mesh fitting on an RTX 4070 Ti SUPER, or 49 fps with parts and contact enabled. The smallest ViT-S model reaches 125 fps without mesh fitting, parts or contact. Code: https://github.com/noahcao/FAH.

</details>


### [54] [Selective Event Evidence and Persistent Temporal Priors for Event-Based Point Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07713)
*Xiaokai Song,Zhang Li,Leqi Liu,Yibin Ye,Kun Wang,Xichao Teng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEPT 是一种纯事件相机长时任意点跟踪框架，通过自适应事件加权和跨窗口轨迹先验减轻稀疏观测及漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件信号稀疏且随场景和运动变化，导致点对应关系难以建立；长序列中的轨迹估计还容易累积漂移。

Method: 在 ETAP 架构上加入 TEEM 和 PTP：TEEM 仅利用跟踪监督学习有界可靠性场，在特征提取前对事件张量的空间、时间及通道维度加权；PTP 对固定局部二次轨迹模型使用舒尔补边缘化，在重叠窗口间传递几何信息，并通过可见性感知修正及截断末端速度初始化新时间点坐标。

Result: 仅使用合成 EventKubric 数据训练，在五个数据集上评估；相较重新训练的 ETAP，SEPT 在任意点跟踪的平均 Jaccard 及特征跟踪的特征年龄、期望特征年龄指标上更优，并在 EC 和 EDS 上超过若干事件与帧融合方法。摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 任务自适应事件证据加权与持久跨窗口轨迹约束相结合，有助于提升纯事件长时点跟踪表现；具体提升幅度及各模块贡献需参阅论文。

Abstract: Event cameras enable tracking under fast motion and challenging illumination. However, stable long-term point tracking remains challenging: event activity is sparse and varies with scene content and motion, complicating correspondence, while trajectory estimates can drift over long sequences. Together, these challenges motivate a tracker that selectively uses informative event evidence and retains trajectory constraints across temporal windows. We propose Selective Event Evidence and Persistent Temporal Priors (SEPT), an event-only framework for tracking any point (TAP) over long sequences. SEPT introduces two complementary modules: Task-Adaptive Event Evidence Modulation (TEEM) and Persistent Trajectory Prior (PTP). Using only tracking supervision, TEEM learns a bounded reliability field that weights the event tensor across space, time, and channels before feature extraction. PTP carries geometric information across overlapping windows by marginalizing a fixed local quadratic trajectory model with a Schur complement. Its visibility-aware correction refines overlap coordinates, while a clipped terminal velocity initializes coordinates at newly exposed timestamps. SEPT builds on the ETAP architecture. SEPT is trained exclusively on synthetic EventKubric data and evaluated on five datasets across TAP and feature tracking. Results show that SEPT outperforms the retrained ETAP baseline in average Jaccard for TAP and in feature age and expected feature age for feature tracking; it also surpasses several methods combining event and frame inputs on EC and EDS. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/sssxxxkkkk/SEPT.

</details>


### [55] [Multimodal Information Interactions Bottleneck for Multimodal Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07718)
*Liangjian Wen,Jiangtao Zheng,Jiang Duan,Qun Dai,Yong Dai,Jianzhuang Liu,Jun Wang,Shu-Dong Huang,Xinwang Liu,Zenglin Xu,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIIB 基于部分信息分解，利用双路径架构和熵代理目标学习多模态中的冗余、独有与协同信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比学习通常依赖多视图冗余假设，侧重共享语义，忽略模态独有信息及跨模态协同，难以充分应对模态异质性和信息量差异。

Method: 提出多模态信息交互瓶颈（MIIB）：采用模态特定编码与多模态融合的双路径架构；结合变分界和基于分数的熵估计，构造可计算的熵代理目标，在无需显式估计互信息的情况下学习最小充分表示。

Result: 摘要称，MIIB 在多个不同基准上持续优于现有先进的多模态自监督方法，但未提供具体数据或基准名称。

Conclusion: 显式建模冗余、独有和协同信息，同时抑制冗余的非必要信息，有助于改进多模态表示；具体效果及适用范围仍需结合论文实验细节判断。

Abstract: Multimodal self-supervised learning is challenged by heterogeneity, unequal informativeness, and complex information interactions across multimodal data sources such as images, text, audio, and tabular data. While contrastive methods effectively align shared semantics, they typically rely on strong multi-view redundancy assumptions, overlooking modality-specific and synergistic information, thus leading to suboptimal representations. We propose the Multimodal Information Interactions Bottleneck (MIIB), a principled framework grounded in Partial Information Decomposition that explicitly encodes redundant, unique, and synergistic information within multimodal interactions. MIIB employs a dual-path model architecture for modality-specific encoding and multimodal fusion, optimized using surrogate tractable entropy-based objectives that enforce minimal sufficient representations without requiring explicit mutual information estimation. By leveraging variational bounds and score-based entropy estimation, MIIB efficiently captures complex cross-modal interactions while suppressing superfluous information. Experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art multimodal self-supervised methods. Code is available at https://github.com/wanglingzaishi/MIIB.

</details>


### [56] [BiGrounder: Bidirectional Training Unlocks the Potential of GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07765)
*Dengyao Shi,Tianyu Fu,Fei Hu,Anyang Su,Hanning Wang,Shuo Wang,Minghui Wu,Chenxu Zhao,Zhen Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出双向奖励训练范式，通过模型自生成指令变体和循环一致性门控，提升GUI定位对语言变化的适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅以最终坐标监督，训练集中每个目标对应的描述有限，难以覆盖真实场景中的多样表达；强化学习前探索能力不足也会限制后续收益。

Method: 监督微调阶段联合学习指令到坐标和坐标到动作描述的双向映射；强化学习阶段在线采样语义一致的指令变体，再将其定位回目标坐标，并通过循环一致性门控关联语义质量与定位成功率。

Result: 摘要报告，4B参数模型在五个GUI定位基准上取得有竞争力的准确率，但未提供具体数值或比较对象。

Conclusion: 将模型自身的指令生成策略转化为持续更新的强化学习轨迹，是改善GUI定位训练的有效方向；具体收益幅度仍需查阅正文验证。

Abstract: Graphical User Interface (GUI) grounding aims to map natural language instructions to precise operational coordinates, serving as a critical task for autonomous agent interaction. Current approaches formalize GUI grounding as predicting action-coordinate pairs from instructions, with end-to-end training supervised solely by final coordinates. Training datasets typically provide only a small number of descriptions per target coordinate, whereas real-world scenarios involve a much larger set of varied descriptions for the same target, which limits robustness to linguistic variation. While recent GUI grounding paradigms have begun employing reinforcement learning to enhance localization accuracy, models with limited exploratory capability before reinforcement yield constrained improvements even with identical training data during the reinforcement phase. To overcome these limitations, we propose a bidirectional reward paradigm that enables model self-reinforcement. During SFT, bidirectional training equips the model to generate action descriptions from target coordinates. During RL, the model samples semantically consistent instruction variations on-policy and grounds each variation back to its target. A cycle-consistency gate couples semantic quality to grounding success, turning the model's own instruction policy into a source of continually refreshed reinforcement trajectories. Our 4B-parameter model achieves competitive accuracy across five grounding benchmarks in our evaluation, indicating the effectiveness of the proposed training paradigm. https://github.com/Spell-shi/BiGrounder

</details>


### [57] [Rain Beyond Streaks: Cascaded Realistic Rain and Wet Surface Synthesis via Scene Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07793)
*Melissa Tijink,Kirill Tolstobrov,Shunxin Wang,Estefania Talavera,Duc V. Le,Luuk Spreeuwers,Shaodi You,Nicola Strisciuglio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CascadeRS 利用深度与语义分割先验，零样本生成兼具雨纹、反射和积水的逼真雨天城市场景，并构建数据集以研究视觉任务的雨天鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雨景合成方法多关注雨纹，忽略反射和积水等表面效应，限制了场景真实感及下游视觉鲁棒性评估。

Method: 提出受物理启发的零样本、可控生成流水线，结合深度和语义分割先验，生成与场景结构及语义一致的雨纹、反射和积水。

Result: 论文报告通过用户研究和定量评估验证了较高的感知真实感，并发布 Cityscapes-CascadeRS 数据集；摘要未提供具体指标或对比数值。

Conclusion: CascadeRS 为大规模、逼真的雨景生成及恶劣雨天视觉鲁棒性评估与提升提供了框架；具体性能增益需参阅正文。

Abstract: Realistic rain synthesis is a persistent challenge for vision systems operating in adverse weather. Existing methods primarily focus on generating rain streaks, often neglecting consequent surface effects such as reflections and puddles, which are critical for perceptual realism and robustness of downstream vision tasks. We introduce CascadeRS, a rain synthesis framework that leverages depth and segmentation priors to render scene-consistent rain and surface effects. In addition to rain streaks, CascadeRS produces reflections and puddles aligned with scene semantics through a physics-inspired zero-shot, controllable generative pipeline. It produces photorealistic rainy urban scenes, with high perceptual realism, which we validate qualitatively with an extensive user study and quantitatively. We produce the Cityscapes-CascadeRS dataset, to study downstream task performance under real rainy conditions. Our results establish CascadeRS as a scalable and realistic rain-generation framework for evaluating and enhancing the robustness of vision in adverse rainy weather. Code and dataset are available via https://github.com/nis-research/CascadeRainSynthesis

</details>


### [58] [Self-Supervised Diffusion Models for Handwritten Signature Verification via Ruin Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07810)
*Peirong Zhang,Zhenhua Yang,Jiaxin Zhang,Moises Diaz,Miguel A. Ferrer,Umapada Pal,Wei Zhou,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SigDiffuser利用严重破坏后的签名重建作为自监督任务，学习更具判别力的签名表示，并提升手写签名验证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手写签名验证主要依赖监督学习，自监督学习在签名表示中的潜力尚未充分探索。

Method: 提出基于扩散模型的自监督框架：通过严重褶皱破坏签名，以完整区域的笔迹特征引导去噪器重建受损部分；采用金字塔特征聚合模块，对视觉编码器特征进行层次聚合与调制。

Result: 摘要称，在多语言签名数据集上，该方法显著优于现有先进自监督学习方法和签名验证模型；增加褶皱破坏程度能够改善表示质量，但未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 结合自监督扩散模型，极端破坏可能比传统轻微变形更有利于学习签名表示，并为其他生物识别任务提供研究方向。

Abstract: Handwritten signature verification (HSV) is a critical biometric technique in legal and forensic authentication, whose efficacy hinges on the discriminability of signature representations. Current HSV methods predominantly adopt supervised learning to enhance representation quality, leaving self-supervised learning largely unexplored. We propose \emph{SigDiffuser}, a diffusion-based self-supervised framework for signature representation and verification. At its core, \emph{SigDiffuser} introduces a novel pretext task termed \emph{Reconstruction from Ruins (RFR)}, which deliberately ``ruins'' signatures through severe crumpling, obliterating their structural integrity and writing styles. A visual encoder is employed to capture crucial handwriting patterns from intact signature regions, guiding the denoiser to reconstruct the ``ruined'' portions and learning meaningful representations. Additionally, we introduce a \emph{pyramidal feature aggregation module (PFAM)} to bridge the visual encoder and denoiser, enhancing representation quality via hierarchical aggregation and modulation. Extensive experiments on multilingual signature datasets demonstrate that \emph{SigDiffuser} significantly outperforms both state-of-the-art SSL methods and HSV models. We further show that increasing the crumpling severity on signatures effectively improves representation quality. Notably, our work demonstrates that extreme destruction, coupled with self-supervised diffusion models, can yield superior signature representation compared to traditional mild deformations, paving new avenues for HSV and other biometric domains. Code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [59] [MV-Sign: Multi-View 3D Motion Integration and Enhanced Text-to-Motion Conditioning for Sign Language Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07811)
*Singyu Muk,Kaustubh Kundu,Kunyuan Xie,Weijie Wang,Kalin Stefanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文通过多视角高质量三维数据与增强文本条件的生成模型，提升手语动作生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维手语生成依赖单视角视频拟合的伪三维数据，易受自遮挡影响并丢失手部细节，同时文本条件约束不足。

Method: 构建跨视角融合并过滤非手语片段的数据集 How2Sign-MV3D；采用手语感知时空 VQ-VAE 联合编码身体与手部动作，并结合伪词汇适配器和关键帧感知检索增强文本与动作对齐。

Result: 在 How2Sign 基准上，相比最先进方法，身体和手部的 DTW-PA-JPE 分别降低 32.8% 和 20.9%。

Conclusion: 多视角数据与改进的文本条件建模可提升三维手语生成表现；论文还发布 ASLCitizen-3D、MM-WAuslan-MV3D 及多视角视频基准 SGNify-MV。

Abstract: Sign language production (SLP) aims to generate appropriate sign language motion from spoken language text. However, current 3D SLP methods suffer from noisy supervision and weak text conditioning. Specifically, these methods typically rely on pseudo-3D motion data fitted from single-view frontal videos, which introduces severe self-occlusion and loses fine-grained hand details, ultimately degrading downstream production. To overcome these limitations, we propose a comprehensive framework that jointly integrates multi-view 3D motion data and optimizes the text-conditioning pathway. First, we introduce How2Sign-MV3D, a high-fidelity dataset constructed via a novel cross-view curation pipeline that fuses multi-view estimates to resolve occlusions and filter non-signing segments. Second, we design a robust SLP model incorporating a sign-aware spatio-temporal VQ-VAE (ST-VQVAE) for joint body-hand tokenization, a pseudo-gloss adapter, and keyframe-aware retrieval to enhance text-to-motion alignment. Evaluated on the How2Sign benchmark, our framework reduces body and hand DTW-PA-JPE by 32.8% and 20.9% respectively compared to the state-of-the-art. Finally, we release two new high-quality 3D datasets, ASLCitizen-3D and MM-WAuslan-MV3D, alongside SGNify-MV as a multi-view video benchmark to support broader community research.

</details>


### [60] [CMDiff: Left Atrial Scar Segmentation from 3D LGE MRI Using Conditional Mask Diffusion Model: A Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07814)
*Jing Zhang,Bastien Bergere,Jonas Leite,Emilie Bollache,Mikaël Laredo,Alban Redheuil,Nadjia Kachenoura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CMDiff可用于左心房瘢痕分割，但在60例数据的五折交叉验证中未超过确定性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LGE心脏MRI中的左心房瘢痕分割面临低对比度、心房壁薄、标注稀缺和类别极度不平衡等困难；条件扩散模型在稀疏三维瘢痕分割中的适用性尚不明确。

Method: 提出条件掩膜扩散模型CMDiff，采用动态编码器条件化的三维正弦UNet预测干净掩膜；在60例LASCARQS公开数据上进行五折交叉验证，每折训练48例、留出12例，并在相同病例上与确定性基线比较。

Result: CMDiff的DSC为0.47±0.08、HD95为12.89±6.39 mm、ASD为1.91±0.96 mm；基线分别为0.51±0.08、10.86±5.39 mm和1.58±1.03 mm。在本研究的相同实现条件下，干净掩膜预测明显优于所评估的噪声预测配置。

Conclusion: 直接掩膜扩散在技术上可行，但其DSC较低、边界距离误差较高，未带来有竞争力的性能提升。代码：https://github.com/jizhang02/CMDiff

Abstract: Late gadolinium enhancement (LGE) cardiac MRI enables non-invasive assessment of left atrial (LA) scar, an imaging marker associated with atrial fibrillation ablation outcome. However, automatic scar segmentation is hindered by low contrast, the thin atrial wall, scarce labels, and extreme class imbalance. Conditional diffusion offers iterative mask generation, but its suitability for sparse 3D LA scar segmentation remains unclear. To address this gap, we propose Conditional Mask Diffusion (CMDiff), which predicts clean masks using a dynamic-encoder-conditioned 3D sinusoidal UNet. We compared CMDiff with a deterministic baseline on identical cases in five-fold cross-validation of 60 scar-labelled public LASCARQS subjects, with 48 training and 12 held-out cases per fold. CMDiff achieved a scar DSC of 0.47 $\pm$ 0.08, HD95 of 12.89 $\pm$ 6.39 mm, and ASD of 1.91 $\pm$ 0.96 mm, compared with deterministic baseline values of 0.51 $\pm$ 0.08, 10.86 $\pm$ 5.39 mm, and 1.58 $\pm$ 1.03 mm, respectively. Under the same implementation, clean-mask prediction performed substantially better than the noise-prediction configuration evaluated in this study. Thus, CMDiff was technically feasible but did not improve segmentation performance: DSC was lower, and both HD95 and ASD were higher. Direct mask diffusion therefore provides an analytical alternative, but not a competitive performance advance for this task. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/jizhang02/CMDiff.

</details>


### [61] [STAG-Net: Structure-Aware Attention over Landmark Groups for Lightweight Head Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07828)
*Asiri Gawesha Lindamulage,Sanka Mohottala,Dharshana Kasthurirathna,Nuwan Kodagoda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STAG-Net 利用结构感知的面部关键点分组与注意力机制，以极低计算成本实现高精度头部姿态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头部姿态估计对人机交互和驾驶员监测重要，但现有图像方法计算开销较高，不利于资源受限场景部署。

Method: 基于面部关键点进行运动感知选择和空间聚类，使用紧凑型 DeepSet 编码器、自注意力与全局几何描述符回归头部姿态；构建 FaceMesh 和 FAN 关键点两个版本，在跨数据集和主体独立协议下评估。

Result: 所有评估设置中，关键点方法比较下 STAG-Net 的平均绝对误差最低；在 BIWI 有效检测帧上，FAN 版本跨数据集和主体独立评估分别达到 3.51° 和 2.39°，均为所比较方法中的最低总体误差。回归器参数量为 0.012–0.043M，计算量为 24.1–83.6K FLOPs；包含关键点提取的 FaceMesh 完整流程在 Tesla T4 上达到 322.9 FPS、CPU 上达到 111.0 FPS。训练样本吞吐量经数据集规模归一化后为 FSA-Net 的 7–8 倍，完整训练不超过 28 分钟。

Conclusion: 该方法在所比较的关键点方法中兼顾领先精度与低训练、推理成本；但回归器 FLOPs 不包含关键点提取，BIWI 最优结果也限定于有效检测帧。

Abstract: Head pose estimation is important for human computer interaction and driver monitoring, but many image based methods remain too costly for resource constrained deployment. We propose STAG-Net, a structure-aware attention over landmark groups for lightweight head Pose estimation that estimates head pose from facial landmarks using movement aware selection, spatial clustering, compact DeepSet encoders, self attention, and a global geometry descriptor. We develop variants using FaceMesh and Face Alignment Network landmarks and evaluate them under cross dataset and subject independent protocols on 300W-LP, AFLW2000, and BIWI. STAG-Net achieves the lowest mean absolute error among all compared landmark based methods in every evaluated setting. On BIWI frames with valid detections, the FAN variant also records the lowest overall error among all compared methods, reaching $3.51^\circ$ under cross dataset evaluation and $2.39^\circ$ under subject independent evaluation. The regressors use only 0.012 to 0.043 million parameters and 24.1 to 83.6 thousand FLOPs, about three orders of magnitude fewer FLOPs than FSA-Net. Despite including landmark extraction and regression, the FaceMesh pipeline reaches 322.9 frames per second on a Tesla T4 GPU, nearly twice the 164.5 frames per second of the fastest measured image based baseline, and 111.0 frames per second on CPU. After normalization by dataset size, STAG-Net processes training samples seven to eight times faster than FSA-Net and more than one hundred times faster than HopeNet and TokenHPE, while complete training finishes within 28 minutes. These results show that STAG-Net combines leading landmark based accuracy with substantially lower training and inference cost.

</details>


### [62] [THG-Omni: Traceable Human-Centric Graph Supervision for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07836)
*Jisheng Dang,Kai Wei,Ronghao Lin,Bimei Wang,Hong Peng,Wei-Shi Zheng,Bin Hu,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: THG-Omni通过以人物为中心的图监督，将视频中的人物轨迹、事件及时间信息转化为结构化证据，提升多模态视频推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态模型通常无法明确指出答案所依据的人物、事件或时间段；仅使用答案级监督也不足以使模型学会理解结构化视频证据。

Method: 结合Segment Anything Model 3的人物轨迹与Qwen3-VL的时间定位标注构建图；利用原视频及可用音频核验候选语义描述，以轨迹几何信息提供时空监督，并生成涵盖属性、动作、情绪、交互及时间的问题。推理时使用从评测视频预测的图。

Result: 在IntentBench的社会推理、情绪理解和欺骗感知任务上，相比未适配的骨干模型均有提升。NExT-QA上，匹配图输入准确率为89.54%，无图输入为85.10%。另一验证实验中，紧凑图、完整图和无图输入准确率分别为78.22%、78.02%和77.58%。

Conclusion: 结构化人物事件图可提供可定位的推理证据并改善视频问答；诊断实验显示，即使没有原视频和音频，预测图仍保留了有助于回答问题的信息。摘要未说明不同实验设置间的完整差异。

Abstract: Omni-modal models can answer questions about video, but their predictions rarely show which participant, event, or interval supports an answer. Moreover, answer-level supervision alone does not teach a model to interpret structured video evidence. To systematically mitigate this issue, we present THG-Omni, a human-centric graph-supervision framework that links Segment Anything Model~3 participant tracks with temporally grounded Qwen3-VL annotations. Candidate semantic claims are checked against the source video and available audio before they are used to construct training questions, while track geometry provides explicit spatial and temporal supervision. The resulting questions teach an omni-modal reasoning model to interpret participant attributes, actions, affect, interactions, and their timing from a structured graph. At inference, THG-Omni uses a predicted graph constructed from the evaluation video. Experiments on IntentBench show consistent improvements over the unadapted backbone across social reasoning, emotion understanding, and deception perception. On NExT-QA, THG-Omni achieves 89.54\% accuracy with the matched graph, compared with 85.10\% without graph input. Further validation experiments show that compact graph input achieves 78.22\% accuracy, compared with 78.02\% using the full graph and 77.58\% without graph input. A complementary diagnostic demonstrates that the predicted graph preserves evidence useful for answering questions even without the source video and audio. Together, these findings show that THG-Omni improves human-centric video reasoning while grounding its evidence in identifiable participants and events. Our codes are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/review-n94hhfcwp5666/

</details>


### [63] [Enhanced MRI brain tumor detection using deep learning in conjunction with explainable AI SHAP based diverse and multi feature analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07843)
*Asif Rahman,Maqsood Hayat,Nadeem Iqbal,Fawaz Khaled Alarfaj,Salem Alkhalaf,Fahad Alturise*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究比较多种MRI特征表示与分类算法；小数据集上SVC准确率为98.06%，大数据集上CNN达到98.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升脑肿瘤MRI自动识别的准确性，并评估不同特征表示及模型的泛化能力。

Method: 比较LBP、Gabor滤波、离散小波变换、快速傅里叶变换、CNN及灰度游程矩阵等特征，以及k近邻、随机森林、SVC、概率神经网络和CNN；通过卡方检验、p值及SHAP分析特征贡献。

Result: 小数据集上CNN和SVC准确率分别为97.8%和98.06%；大型基准数据集上分别为98.9%和96.7%。LBP结合SVC和CNN表现出较高的准确率和特异度。

Conclusion: LBP特征具有识别价值，CNN在大型基准数据集上表现最佳；摘要未提供数据集规模、划分方式及外部验证细节，临床泛化能力仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Recent innovations in medical imaging have markedly improved brain tumor identification, surpassing conventional diagnostic approaches that suffer from low resolution, radiation exposure, and limited contrast. Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is pivotal in precise and accurate tumor characterization owing to its high-resolution, non-invasive nature. This study investigates the synergy among multiple feature representation schemes such as local Binary Patterns (LBP), Gabor filters, Discrete Wavelet Transform, Fast Fourier Transform, Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN), and Gray-Level Run Length Matrix alongside five learning algorithms namely: k-nearest Neighbor, Random Forest, Support Vector Classifier (SVC), and probabilistic neural network (PNN), and CNN. Empirical findings indicate that LBP in conjunction with SVC and CNN obtained high specificity and accuracy, rendering it a promising method for MRI-based tumor diagnosis. Further to investigate the contribution of LBP, Statistical analysis chi-square and p-value tests are used to confirm the significant impact of LBP feature space for identification of brain Tumor. In addition, The SHAP analysis was used to identify the most important features in classification. In a small dataset, CNN obtained 97.8% accuracy while SVC yielded 98.06% accuracy. In subsequent analysis, a large benchmark dataset is also utilized to evaluate the performance of learning algorithms in order to investigate the generalization power of the proposed model. CNN achieves the highest accuracy of 98.9%, followed by SVC at 96.7%. These results highlight CNN's effectiveness in automated, high-precision tumor diagnosis. Code: https://github.com/asifrahman557/BrainTumorDetection

</details>


### [64] [OcTreeMamba: Topology-Aware Mamba for 3D Medical Segmentation via Octree Sparsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07865)
*Kaixiang Mai,Jiaqi Yu,Yuzhu Ji,Yiqun Zhang,Zhijie Wang,Haijun Zhang,Yiu-ming Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OcTreeMamba 将八叉树与状态空间模型结合，利用解剖结构自适应稀疏化和树形双向特征传播，实现兼顾拓扑连续性与计算效率的三维医学图像分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Mamba 方法的启发式选择性扫描可能破坏三维空间层次、产生计算冗余，并忽略解剖结构固有的稀疏性。

Method: 结构学习器通过层次门控和 Gumbel-Softmax 采样，可微地选择解剖区域；动态八叉树构建器将选中区域组织为保持层次连接的多级拓扑；双向 Tree-SSM 沿叶节点至根节点及反向路径传播和融合上下文特征。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在拓扑连续性和计算效率方面具有竞争力，尤其有助于恢复血管数据集中的复杂结构；摘要未提供具体量化指标。

Conclusion: 将结构自适应稀疏化、八叉树拓扑与状态空间模型结合，是实现拓扑感知的高效三维分割的一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of 3D anatomical structures is crucial to medical image analysis. Recently, Mamba-based approaches have achieved promising segmentation performance in capturing long-range dependencies with linear-time computational complexity. However, heuristic selective scanning strategies inherently disrupt the intrinsic spatial hierarchy and increase computational redundancy, ignoring the inherent sparsity of 3D anatomical structures. To this end, we propose OcTreeMamba, a hierarchical framework that incorporates Octree structures into SSM, achieving 3D topology-aware long-range context feature propagation and integration. Specifically, a Structure Learner performs anatomy-adaptive structured sparsification through hierarchical gating and Gumbel-Softmax sampling, enabling differentiable selection of anatomical regions. The selected regions are subsequently organized by a Dynamic Octree Builder into a multi-level topology that preserves their hierarchical connectivity. Based on the constructed Octree, a bidirectional Tree-SSM propagates and integrates contextual features along connectivity-aware leaf--parent--root and root--parent--leaf trajectories. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets show that our proposed OcTreeMamba achieves competitive performance \wrt topological continuity and computational efficiency, particularly for recovering the complex structure on vascular datasets. The code is available at https://github.com/MikeMak123/OcTreeMamba.

</details>


### [65] [PlayGen2: Learning Real-Time Playable Worlds with Explicit 3D State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07867)
*Jihai Zhang,Yuhan Zhou,Junyou Li,Deheng Ye,Qi Dou,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PlayGen2将持久化3D世界状态与神经渲染解耦，实现实时、可交互且可扩展的游戏世界模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅以生成的视频帧作为循环世界表示，会将控制、空间记忆和动态交互绑定到视角相关的图像合成，难以维持长期时空一致性。

Method: 使用体素占据网格表示静态几何，并以身份索引的3D状态表示玩家和其他实体；在固定大小的局部区域联合学习动作条件下的实体动力学、几何补全和实体发现，将更新写入持久化世界记忆。独立神经渲染器根据深度和语义投影生成第一人称画面，生成图像不反馈至状态预测。

Result: 在Doom测试中，模型学会了考虑几何约束的玩家运动及玩家与NPC交互；在搭载M4 Pro芯片的MacBook Pro上，完整模拟与渲染流程的后端延迟约为160毫秒。还展示了未见地图的零样本部署、通过组合单人模型实现多人交互，以及通过基于规则的状态干预实现可编程玩法。

Conclusion: 将世界状态演化与渲染分离，有助于构建具备迁移性和可扩展性的交互式世界模型；论文公开了代码和预训练权重。摘要未提供定量对比或长期一致性的具体评测。

Abstract: Building a playable world requires more than generating plausible video: it demands responsive control, persistent spatial structure, and coherent interactions with dynamic entities. Using generated frames as the sole recurrent world representation ties these requirements to view-dependent appearance synthesis, making it difficult to maintain long-term spatial and temporal consistency in generation and interaction. We present PlayGen2, a real-time interactive world model that separates learned 3D state evolution from neural rendering. PlayGen2 maintains a persistent world memory comprising static geometry represented by a volumetric occupancy grid and identity-indexed 3D states for the player and other entities. Within a fixed-size local region, its world-state model jointly learns action-conditioned entity dynamics, geometry completion, and entity discovery, committing updates to a persistent world memory. A separate neural renderer synthesizes first-person observations from depth and semantic projections of the world state, without feeding generated images back into state prediction. Using Doom as a testbed, we demonstrate that the model successfully learns world dynamics including geometry-aware player motion and player--NPC interactions. The framework is computationally efficient, enabling the complete simulation and rendering pipeline to achieve approximately 160ms backend latency on a MacBook Pro with an M4 Pro chip. Beyond single-player interaction, we demonstrate zero-shot deployment on unseen Doom maps, multiplayer interaction through the composition of single-player models, and programmable gameplay through rule-based state interventions. These capabilities demonstrate the transferability and extensibility of the learned game-world model. Code and pretrained model weights are publicly available.

</details>


### [66] [PVDepth: Panoramic Video Depth Estimation via Geometry-Aware Spatiotemporal Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07873)
*Chuanxin Song,Peixi Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出全景视频深度数据集 PanoCARLA 和端到端框架 PVDepth，以改善全景深度估计的几何精度与时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模全景视频训练数据匮乏；等距柱状投影（ERP）的几何畸变和非线性时间动态阻碍透视视频深度模型直接迁移。

Method: 构建包含自然运动轨迹和无人机式漫游视角的合成 RGB-D 全景视频数据集；在透视视频深度模型基础上，引入渐进式球面感知噪声初始化，以及利用辅助立方体贴图分支的时间建模模块。

Result: 摘要报告 PVDepth 在实验中表现优越，生成的深度序列具有较好的几何精度和时间一致性；未提供具体数值或对比基线。

Conclusion: 数据集与针对 ERP 特性的模型设计共同支持全景视频深度估计；具体性能提升及泛化能力仍需结合论文实验验证。

Abstract: Panoramic video depth estimation is pivotal for applications such as Virtual Reality and World Models. However, advancements in this field are impeded by two primary obstacles: the scarcity of large-scale training data and the unique spatiotemporal challenges of Equirectangular Projection (ERP), which hinder the direct transfer of perspective models. In this paper, we first present PanoCARLA, a large-scale synthetic RGB-D panoramic video dataset, featuring natural motion trajectories and drone-like roaming perspectives. Building on this foundation, we propose PVDepth, an end-to-end framework adapted from perspective video depth models. To tackle ERP-specific geometric distortions and consequent non-linear temporal dynamics, we introduce two core mechanisms: (1) A Progressive Sphere-aware Noise Initialization strategy that anneals the noise distribution from planar to spherical, guiding the model to adapt to non-uniform information density; and (2) A Cube-rectified Temporal Modeling module that incorporates an auxiliary cubemap temporal branch to rectify non-linear temporal dynamics in the ERP domain. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PVDepth achieves superior performance, generating geometrically accurate and temporally consistent depth sequences. Code and data will be released at https:// github.com/ChuanxinSong/PVDepth.

</details>


### [67] [InnerLogic-Surg: An Open Surgical Foundation Model via Continual Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07883)
*Kun Yuan,Hao Ding,Yiqing Shen,Gasper Podobnik,Benjamin Killeen,Tito Porras,Axel Krieger,Mathias Unberath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InnerLogic-Surg通过大规模手术图像持续预训练，构建可迁移的手术视觉编码器，在20项手术任务的线性探测评估中均取得最佳表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术图像分析系统多依赖自然图像预训练编码器，或使用相对较小数据集从头训练的专用模型；作者希望通过大规模手术领域预训练，获得更强且通用的视觉骨干。

Method: 基于Transformer图像编码器，沿用DINOv3预训练阶段并进行手术领域持续预训练；数据来自11,965段互联网手术视频，约2,900小时，覆盖15种以上术式。模型参数规模为2,100万至3亿，并采用可学习的特征上采样模块，以较低令牌成本支持高分辨率密集预测。

Result: 在同架构比较下，最大模型在涵盖工作流程、器械与解剖结构识别、分割、检测和单目深度估计的全部20项手术任务中，线性探测表现最佳。

Conclusion: 手术领域持续预训练可产生跨术式、解剖区域和任务类型迁移的表征，为传统针对特定术式的表征学习提供可扩展的替代方案。摘要未报告具体指标或完整微调结果。

Abstract: Vision foundation models are the backbone of modern surgical image analysis, yet most surgical systems still rely on encoders pretrained on natural images or on specialist models trained from scratch on comparatively small datasets. Pretraining encoders on large amounts of surgery-specific data has the potential to generally enhance the performance of surgical image analysis methods simply by providing stronger backbones. Here we present InnerLogic-Surg, an open family of surgical vision encoders (21M--300M parameters) obtained by continually pretraining a transformer-based image encoder on millions of surgical images curated from 11,965 internet-sourced surgical videos spanning ~2,900 hours and more than fifteen procedure types. Our training recipe inherits DINOv3's pretraining stage to preserve representation ability after surgical adaptation, and additionally applies a learned feature-upsampling module for high-resolution dense prediction at low token cost. Across a benchmark covering workflow, instrument and anatomy recognition, segmentation, detection, and monocular depth, under the same architecture, the largest InnerLogic-Surg model achieves the best linear-probe performance in all 20 surgical tasks. We demonstrate that surgical continual pretraining yields transferable representations that generalize across procedures, anatomical regions, and task types, showing our surgical image encoders as a promising and scalable alternative to the procedure-specific representation learning approaches that have traditionally dominated surgical computer vision.

</details>


### [68] [Structure from Collision via Hypothesis-Space Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07900)
*Takuhiro Kaneko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过显式空腔假设、无梯度全局搜索与可微局部优化，从碰撞多视角观测中重建物体内部结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空腔在静态表面没有直接可见特征；基于密度的优化难以从表面匹配梯度定位空腔，且空腔附近的密度梯度可能较弱或缺乏信息。

Method: 将空腔表示为编码结构先验的显式假设；先通过仅前向物质点法（MPM）模拟进行无梯度全局搜索，再将选定假设表示为拉格朗日粒子系统，利用可微MPM进行梯度局部优化。

Result: 在SfC基准上，内部结构重建和未来预测均优于基于密度的SfC基线；内部拓扑压力测试与稳健性分析探讨了适用范围和局限。

Conclusion: 显式假设空间推理结合全局搜索和局部可微优化，为从碰撞动态推断隐藏空腔提供了有效方法；摘要未提供具体数值或局限细节。

Abstract: Knowledge of the hidden interior structure of an object, including the presence and location of cavities, is essential for predictive simulation, robotic manipulation, and nondestructive inspection. However, such cavities leave no direct signature on the static visible surface, making collision-induced deformation one of the few cues used to infer their presence. Structure from Collision (SfC) addresses this problem by recovering the hidden interior structure of an object from multiview observations of a collision event. SfC is challenging for two reasons: hidden cavities are only indirectly observable through surface motion, and voids correspond to the absence of material, making direct optimization using standard density representations difficult. The density-based SfC baseline inherits both difficulties. Surface-matching gradients do not directly indicate the cavity location, while density gradients can be weak or uninformative inside or near void regions. Therefore, we reformulate SfC as hypothesis-space reasoning, representing hidden cavities as explicit structural hypotheses whose variables encode available structural assumptions rather than relying on implicit density updates. A global stage performs gradient-free optimization over this hypothesis space using forward-only material point method (MPM) simulation. A local stage then refines the selected hypothesis as a Lagrangian particle system through gradient-based optimization using differentiable MPM simulation. On the SfC benchmark, our method improves hidden-structure reconstruction and future prediction over the density-based SfC baseline. Interior-topology stress tests and robustness analyses characterize its scope and limitations. The project page is available at https://www.kecl.ntt.co.jp/people/kaneko.takuhiro/projects/sfc-hypothesis/.

</details>


### [69] [XM4D: 4D Reconstruction through Certifiable Geometric Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07902)
*Haoyu Han,Jack Benarroch Jedlicki,Jiahui Lei,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: XM4D 将四维重建拆分为相机运动估计与非刚性重建，通过可验证的全局优化和动态高斯泼溅实现高精度重建与新视角渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机运动、场景几何和非刚性形变的联合估计高度非凸，难以保证全局最优，同时需要兼顾重建精度与计算效率。

Method: 利用基础深度预测模型将静态点轨迹提升至三维，构建联合估计相机位姿、深度尺度和静态路标的尺度化光束平差；解析消去平移和路标，得到关于带尺度旋转的 QCQP。非刚性运动采用嵌入式形变图，其跟踪、刚性和时间正则化目标同样化为仅含旋转的 QCQP。两阶段均通过半定规划松弛及基于黎曼优化的 GPU 加速 Burer–Monteiro 求解器求解，并用恢复的几何初始化动态高斯泼溅进行光度细化。

Result: 在七个基准上，方法在静态和动态场景中均取得准确的相机运动估计，可扩展至数千帧，并实现领先的新视角渲染质量；摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 分阶段的结构化几何优化结合可验证的最优性证书和光度细化，为准确、可扩展的四维重建提供了有效方案；证书针对所求解的优化问题，不等同于对整个重建流程保证全局最优。

Abstract: Accurate 4D reconstruction remains challenging: jointly estimating camera motion, scene geometry, and non-rigid deformation entails highly nonconvex optimization, making global optimality difficult to guarantee. We present XM4D, a framework for accurate and efficient 4D reconstruction built upon certifiably globally optimal geometric optimization. By separating the estimation of camera motion from non-rigid deformation, we formulate both stages as structured quadratically constrained quadratic programs (QCQP) that admit semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxations and verifiable global optimality certificates. For camera motion estimation, we lift static point tracks into 3D using foundational depth prediction models and formulate a scaled bundle adjustment problem that jointly estimates camera poses, depth scales, and static landmarks. By analytically eliminating translations and landmarks, we reduce the problem to a QCQP over scaled rotations. For non-rigid reconstruction, we represent dynamic motion using an embedded deformation graph (EDG) and show that its tracking, rigidity, and temporal regularization objectives similarly reduce to a rotation-only QCQP. Both problems admit SDP relaxations that we solve efficiently with a GPU-accelerated Burer-Monteiro solver based on Riemannian optimization. The recovered geometry further initializes dynamic Gaussian splatting for photometric refinement. Extensive experiments across seven benchmarks demonstrate accurate camera motion estimation on both static and dynamic scenes, efficient optimization that scales to thousands of frames, and state-of-the-art novel-view rendering quality.

</details>


### [70] [StitchEdit: Stitching Depth Priors into Image Editors via Per-Layer Gradient Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07922)
*Qin Guo,Dan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StitchEdit 利用分层梯度探测协调深度估计与图像编辑，在保留几何知识的同时提升空间编辑效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的文本引导编辑难以处理依赖三维结构的空间变换、遮挡和视角变化；如何将预训练模型的几何先验有效转用于编辑仍未解决。

Method: 通过逐层梯度探测发现，编辑与深度估计目标在早期 Transformer 层兼容、在后期层冲突。方法先用轻量级深度去噪适配器激活几何能力，再依据探测结果采用逐层学习率调度，对适配器进行编辑微调。

Result: 在包含150项几何依赖编辑的 StitchEditBench 上，该方法的感知质量和几何保真度均持续优于指令编辑及深度条件编辑基线；摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 有选择地保留或削弱不同层的深度知识，有助于缓解任务冲突，改善几何敏感的图像编辑。

Abstract: Text-guided image editing with diffusion models struggles with spatial edits (e.g. manipulating objects, changing viewpoints, manipulating occlusions) that depend on the 3D structure understanding of a scene. Marigold and follow-up work demonstrate that a pretrained diffusion backbone already harbors strong geometric priors that can be activated for depth estimation, yet how to redirect these priors toward editing remains unresolved. Through per-layer gradient probing, we discover that the editing and depth objectives are compatible in early transformer layers but actively conflict in late layers, explaining why naive depth-conditioned editing either ignores geometry or degrades visual quality. We present StitchEdit, which first activates the backbone's geometric capability via a lightweight depth-denoising adapter, then selectively fine-tunes this adapter for editing using a probe-guided per-layer learning rate schedule that preserves depth knowledge where it helps and prunes it where it conflicts. On the proposed StitchEditBench, a benchmark of 150 geometry-dependent edits spanning spatial transformation, occlusion manipulation, and viewpoint change, StitchEdit consistently surpasses both instruction-based and depth-conditioned baselines on perceptual quality and geometric fidelity.

</details>


### [71] [Towards Scalable Diagnostic-Driven Whole-Slide Image Retrieval for Pathology Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07935)
*Jingsong Liu,Youssef Wally Han Li,Weiwei Zhou,Jing Dai,Elisabeth Wetzer,Peter J. Schüffler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLEAR-WSI利用固定长度切片嵌入和诊断标签精炼，实现可扩展的全切片图像检索，并在四项任务中优于所评估的最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字病理图像档案快速增长，但元数据不一致或缺失；现有检索方法依赖人工设计的索引流程和预定义采样策略，限制了扩展性与泛化能力。

Method: 将全切片图像表示为固定长度嵌入，并根据检索场景使用预测或真实诊断标签精炼候选结果；将新处理的病例加入持续增长的记忆库。

Result: 在两个公开数据集和一个私有临床数据集构成的四项检索任务中，相较最强评估基线，Precision@5在CAMELYON16、BRACS-1、BRACS-2和X-HER2上分别提高3.10、2.75、1.95和3.75；摘要未明确这些数值的单位。消融及延迟实验进一步评估了组件贡献和部署潜力。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了面向诊断的全切片图像检索及可扩展部署潜力；其跨场景泛化能力仍需更多独立验证。

Abstract: The growing adoption of digital pathology has rapidly expanded collections of hematoxylin-and-eosin whole-slide images, yet efficient navigation through these archives remains challenging, as metadata is often inconsistent or unavailable. Content-based image retrieval addresses this challenge by identifying similar cases directly from visual representations instead of manual annotations or metadata. Existing retrieval systems have demonstrated promising performance but largely rely on handcrafted indexing pipelines and predefined sampling strategies, constraining scalability and generalization. Here, we present CLEAR-WSI (Constant-Length Embedding for Automatic Retrieval of Whole-Slide Images) as a scalable, diagnostic-driven slide-retrieval framework, validated on both public and clinical data. Using a fixed-length slide embedding, CLEAR-WSI refines retrieved candidates with either a predicted diagnostic label or the ground-truth label, depending on the retrieval setting. Newly processed cases are subsequently added to the expanding memory bank, allowing the retrieval archive to grow over time. On a comprehensive benchmark consisting of two public and one private clinical datasets representing four retrieval tasks, CLEAR-WSI improves Precision@5 over the strongest evaluated baseline, with gains on CAMELYON16 (3.10), BRACS-1 (2.75), BRACS-2 (1.95), and X-HER2 (3.75). Additional ablation and latency studies further examine the contribution of each component and highlight its potential for scalable deployment.

</details>


### [72] [WAM-Sim0.1: World Anchored Memory Meets Online RL for Consistent and Realistic Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07939)
*Jiahao Cui,Jiaye Li,Wenhao Zhang,Yaoyi Li,Shan Luan,Quanhui Tang,Jianhua Han,Lin Cheng,Hang Xu,Siyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WAM-Sim通过持久分层记忆和在线流匹配强化学习，提升驾驶世界模型长时生成的一致性、视觉质量和物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归驾驶世界模型依赖缺乏持久状态的瞬时条件，长时生成容易出现道路结构漂移、参与者身份漂移和质量衰减。

Method: 构建三层记忆：姿态对齐的结构令牌编码道路拓扑，只读场景令牌提供地理定位的背景外观，一次写入的实例令牌保持交通参与者身份；对自生成轨迹进行在线流匹配强化学习，利用层级对齐视觉奖励和视觉语言模型物理评判器优化。

Result: 在nuScenes上达到FID 3.47、FVD 25.72、感知mAP 24.16和道路mIoU 71.23；在自采集数据的分钟级生成中减轻结构与身份漂移。

Conclusion: 持久记忆为优化提供稳定语境，有助于抑制语义坍塌和奖励投机；在线强化学习进一步改善物理常识与视觉细节。摘要未提供对照数值或完整实验设置。

Abstract: Autoregressive driving world models systematically suffer from structural drift, identity drift, and quality decay over long rollout horizons due to transient conditioning signals that lack persistent state. We present WAM-Sim, a driving world model anchored by a persistent hierarchical memory and optimized via online flow-matching RL. WAM-Sim structures persistent context across three temporal and semantic scales: pose-aligned structural tokens encoding persistent road topology, read-only scene tokens supplying geolocated background appearance, and write-once instance tokens preserving individual traffic participant identities across occlusions and chunk boundaries. To restore visual sharpness and physical plausibility degraded by multi-scale conditioning, we introduce an online post-training framework that optimizes self-generated rollouts via flow-matching RL guided by hierarchy-aligned visual rewards and a vision-language model physical judge. The invariant memory empirically confines policy optimization within the anchored context, helping suppress semantic collapse and reward hacking, while online RL systematically enforces physical commonsense and fine-grained visual fidelity. On the nuScenes benchmark, WAM-Sim achieves a superior generation fidelity (FID=3.47, FVD=25.72) and strong downstream perception performance (24.16 mAP, 71.23 road mIoU). On minute-scale rollouts of self-collected data, WAM-Sim substantially mitigates structural and identity drift, preserving high scene similarity and subject identity coherence across extended horizons. Project page: https://fudan-generative-vision.github.io/WAM-Sim.

</details>


### [73] [IDPrior: Decoupling Identity Generation from Facial Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07941)
*Fadi Boutros,Meriç Demirörs,Darian Tomašević,Vitomir Štruc,Naser Damer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IDPrior通过学习人脸身份嵌入的扩散先验，生成新身份并合成同一身份的多张图像，为人脸识别提供可扩展的合成训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实身份标注的人脸数据收集和标注成本高，并涉及隐私与伦理问题；现有合成方法通常未显式建模人脸身份的潜在分布。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：首先在预训练人脸识别模型的嵌入空间中学习扩散先验，生成新身份嵌入；随后以这些嵌入作为预训练身份条件扩散模型的条件，合成每个身份的多张图像。该设计将身份生成与图像合成解耦。

Result: 实验表明，生成身份具有多样性和良好的身份间可分性，其分布也更接近真实身份分布。基于所生成数据训练的人脸识别模型，在多个验证基准上达到与现有合成数据方法相当或更优的性能；摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 显式建模身份分布能够为合成人脸数据提供一种可扩展、模块化的身份生成方式，并可与现有及未来的身份条件扩散模型结合。

Abstract: Large-scale identity-labeled face datasets are essential for training modern face recognition models, yet collecting and annotating such data is costly and raises privacy and ethical concerns. Existing synthetic face generation methods typically derive identities from predefined class labels, unconditional image generation, or predefined latent sampling strategies, without explicitly modeling the underlying distribution of facial identities. In this paper, we propose IDPrior, a two-stage framework that decouples identity generation from image synthesis. We first learn a diffusion prior in the embedding space of a pretrained face recognition model to model the distribution of facial identities and generate novel identity embeddings. These embeddings are then used to condition a pretrained identity-conditioned diffusion model (DM), enabling the synthesis of multiple images of the same identity. This modular design provides a scalable approach to identity generation while remaining compatible with existing and future identity-conditioned DMs. Experiments indicate that IDPrior generates diverse and well-separated identities while maintaining a closer correspondence to the distribution of authentic identities. Face recognition models trained on datasets generated using IDPrior-generated identities achieve competitive or improved performance compared with existing synthetic-data-based methods across multiple face verification benchmarks. Code and generated datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/fdbtrs/IDperturb.

</details>


### [74] [Rotation Is Token-Blind: A Structural Criterion for Post-Training Quantization of Feed-Forward Geometry Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07955)
*Huanfei He,Bohao Zhang,Jiaxin Zhang,Wei Sui,John See,Zeyd Boukhers,Cong Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QMapAnything 是面向前馈式三维重建模型的结构感知训练后量化框架，针对不同 token 的量化敏感性进行处理，在降低资源消耗的同时保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
